Catalan.cassels_coprime_power_factors : ∀ (n : ℕ),
  Odd n → ∀ (A B C : ℤ), A ≠ 0 → B ≠ 0 → A.gcd B = 1 → A * B = C ^ n → ∃ a b, A = a ^ n ∧ B = b ^ n
Catalan.cassels_cyclo_exact_valuation : ∀ (p : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → p ≠ 2 → ∀ (x : ℤ), ↑p ∣ x - 1 → padicValInt p (Catalan.casselsCyclo p x) = 1
Catalan.cassels_coeff_denominator : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    ¬q ∣ p →
      ∃ B, (∀ (k : ℕ), ¬↑q ∣ B k) ∧ ∀ (k : ℕ), Catalan.casselsCoeff p q k = ↑(B k) / ↑q ^ Catalan.casselsDenExp q k
Catalan.cassels_coeff_padicValRat : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → ¬q ∣ p → ∀ (k : ℕ), padicValRat q (Catalan.casselsCoeff p q k) = -↑(Catalan.casselsDenExp q k)
Catalan.cassels_remainder_bound : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q →
      p ≠ 2 →
        q ≠ 2 →
          q < p →
            ∀ (t : ℝ),
              |t| ≤ 1 / 2 →
                |Catalan.casselsF p q t - Catalan.casselsTaylor p q (Catalan.casselsIndex p q) t| ≤
                  |t| ^ (Catalan.casselsIndex p q + 1) / (1 - |t|) ^ 2
Catalan.cassels_p_dvd_y : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → ↑p ∣ y
Catalan.cassels_q_dvd_x : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → ↑q ∣ x
Catalan.cassels_factorization : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q →
      p ≠ 2 →
        q ≠ 2 →
          ∀ (x y : ℤ),
            x ≠ 0 →
              y ≠ 0 →
                x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
                  ∃ a b u v,
                    a ≠ 0 ∧
                      b ≠ 0 ∧
                        0 < u ∧
                          0 < v ∧
                            x - 1 = ↑p ^ (q - 1) * a ^ q ∧
                              y = ↑p * a * u ∧
                                x ^ p - 1 = (x - 1) * (↑p * u ^ q) ∧
                                  y + 1 = ↑q ^ (p - 1) * b ^ p ∧ x = ↑q * b * v ∧ y ^ q + 1 = (y + 1) * (↑q * v ^ p)
Catalan.ipow_principalIdeal : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (γ : Kˣ)
  (Θ : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.ipow p K ((Catalan.principalIdeal K) γ) Θ =
    (Catalan.principalIdeal K) (Θ.coeff.prod fun τ m => (Catalan.elementAct K τ) γ ^ m)
Catalan.principal_of_integral_factorization : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  {ι : Type u_2} [inst_2 : Fintype ι] (I : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) (hI : I ≠ ⊥) (τ : ι → Catalan.G p K)
  (n : ι → ℕ) (Γ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (hΓ : ↑Γ ≠ 0),
  Ideal.span {Γ} = ∏ i, Ideal.map (Catalan.integerAut K (τ i)).toRingHom I ^ n i →
    Catalan.ipow p K (Catalan.idealUnit K I hI) (∑ i, MonoidAlgebra.single (τ i) ↑(n i)) =
      (Catalan.principalIdeal K) (Units.mk0 (↑Γ) hΓ)

Catalan.ζ_spec : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [hcycl : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K], IsPrimitiveRoot (Catalan.ζ p K) p
Catalan.σ_apply_ζ : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [hcycl : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), (Catalan.σ p K a) (Catalan.ζ p K) = Catalan.ζ p K ^ (↑a).val
Catalan.weight_mul : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (A B : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.weight p K (A * B) = Catalan.weight p K A * Catalan.weight p K B
Catalan.size_mul_le : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (A B : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.size p K (A * B) ≤ Catalan.size p K A * Catalan.size p K B
Catalan.upow_mul : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (a : Kˣ) (A Θ : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.upow p K a (A * Θ) = Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.upow p K a Θ) A
Catalan.fractionalIdeal_prime_induction : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (P : Catalan.FracIdealUnit K → Prop),
  P 1 →
    (∀ (I J : Catalan.FracIdealUnit K), P I → P J → P (I * J)) →
      (∀ (I : Catalan.FracIdealUnit K), P I → P I⁻¹) →
        (∀ (I : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)), I.IsPrime → ∀ (hI0 : I ≠ ⊥), P (Catalan.idealUnit K I hI0)) →
          ∀ (J : Catalan.FracIdealUnit K), P J

  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [hcycl : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (I : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)),
  I.IsPrime → ∀ (hI0 : I ≠ ⊥), ↑p ∈ I → ∃ γ, Catalan.idealUnit K I hI0 = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) γ

Catalan.lambda_integral_coprime_of_prime_dvd : ∀ (p : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    p ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (x : ℤ),
        ↑p ∣ x - 1 →
          ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K]
            [inst_3 : Fact (Nat.Prime p)], ∃ l, ↑l = (↑x - Catalan.ζ p K) / (1 - Catalan.ζ p K) ∧ IsCoprime (↑p) l
@Catalan.ideal_pow_of_root_factor : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] [IsDedekindDomain A] (p q : ℕ) (η x y l : A),
  IsPrimitiveRoot η p →
    0 < p → (1 - η) * l = x - η → IsCoprime (↑p) l → 1 - η ∣ ↑p → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → ∃ a, Ideal.span {l} = a ^ q
Catalan.lambda_ideal_pow : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q →
      p ≠ 2 →
        q ≠ 2 →
          ∀ (x y : ℤ),
            x ≠ 0 →
              y ≠ 0 →
                x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
                  ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K]
                    [inst_3 : Fact (Nat.Prime p)],
                    ∃ l 𝔞, ↑l = (↑x - Catalan.ζ p K) / (1 - Catalan.ζ p K) ∧ Ideal.span {l} = 𝔞 ^ q

Catalan.hyyro_bound : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → ↑p ^ (q - 1) * (↑q - 1) ^ q + 1 ≤ |x|
Catalan.x_sub_ζ_ne_zero : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (x : ℤ), p ≠ 2 → ↑x - Catalan.ζ p K ≠ 0
Catalan.qtorsion_trivial : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)], p ≠ q → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (a : Kˣ), a ^ q = 1 → a = 1
Catalan.mihIdeal : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → ℕ → ℤ → p ≠ 2 → Ideal (Catalan.R p K)
Catalan.mihAug : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → ℕ → ℤ → p ≠ 2 → AddSubgroup (Catalan.R p K)
Catalan.alpha_pow : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihIdeal p K q x hp2)), Catalan.alpha p K q x hp2 Θ ^ q = Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.xmζ p K x hp2) ↑Θ
Catalan.alpha_add : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (Θ Ψ : ↥(Catalan.mihIdeal p K q x hp2)),
        Catalan.alpha p K q x hp2 (Θ + Ψ) = Catalan.alpha p K q x hp2 Θ * Catalan.alpha p K q x hp2 Ψ
Catalan.prime_generator_away_two : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {2} ℚ K] (I : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) (hI0 : I ≠ ⊥) (k : ℕ),
  ∃ γ, Catalan.ipow 2 K (Catalan.idealUnit K I hI0) (Catalan.ΘS 2 K k) = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) γ
Catalan.exists_descended_gauss_quotient : ∀ (K : Type u_1) (E : Type u_2) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : Field E]
  [inst_2 : Algebra K E] [IsGalois K E] [FiniteDimensional K E] (g : E),
  g ≠ 0 →
    ∀ (s : E ≃+* E) (σ : K ≃+* K) (k p : ℕ) (Γ : K),
      (∀ (x : K), s ((algebraMap K E) x) = (algebraMap K E) (σ x)) →
        g ^ p = (algebraMap K E) Γ →
          ∀ (c : Gal(E/K) → E),
            (∀ (τ : Gal(E/K)), τ g = c τ * g) →
              (∀ (τ : Gal(E/K)), s (c τ) = c τ ^ k) →
                (∀ (τ : Gal(E/K)), Function.Commute ⇑τ ⇑s) →
                  ∃ δ, (algebraMap K E) ↑δ = g ^ k / s g ∧ ↑δ ^ p = Γ ^ k / σ Γ
Catalan.stickelberger_from_away_generators : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  (∀ (I : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)),
      I.IsPrime →
        ∀ (hI0 : I ≠ ⊥),
          ↑p ∉ I →
            ∀ (k : ℕ),
              0 < k →
                k ≤ p →
                  ∃ γ, Catalan.ipow p K (Catalan.idealUnit K I hI0) (Catalan.ΘS p K k) = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) γ) →
    ∀ Θ ∈ Catalan.stickSpan p K,
      ∀ (J : Catalan.FracIdealUnit K), ∃ γ, Catalan.ipow p K J Θ = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) γ

Catalan.fractional_generator_of_primes_away_two_mul : ∀ (p : ℕ),

Catalan.ramifiedPrime_facts : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 →
    (Catalan.ramifiedPrime p K).IsPrime ∧
      Catalan.ramifiedPrime p K ≠ ⊥ ∧
        Catalan.ramifiedPrime p K ^ (p - 1) = Ideal.span {↑p} ∧ Ideal.absNorm (Catalan.ramifiedPrime p K) = p
Catalan.difference_ideal : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (s t : Catalan.G p K),
      s ≠ t → Ideal.span {Catalan.zetaConjInt p K s - Catalan.zetaConjInt p K t} = Catalan.ramifiedPrime p K
Catalan.theta_identity : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [hcycl : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (a : (ZMod p)ˣ),
  ↑(↑a).val • Catalan.pθ p K - MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.σ p K a) 1 * Catalan.pθ p K = ↑p • Catalan.ΘS p K (↑a).val
Catalan.theta_principal_of_gauss_quotient : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (J : Catalan.FracIdealUnit K) (a : (ZMod p)ˣ)
  (Γ δ : Kˣ),
  Catalan.ipow p K J (Catalan.pθ p K) = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) Γ →
    δ ^ p = Γ ^ (↑a).val / (Catalan.elementAct K (Catalan.σ p K a)) Γ →
      Catalan.ipow p K J (Catalan.ΘS p K (↑a).val) = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) δ
Catalan.norm_x_sub_zeta : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (x : ℤ),
  (Algebra.norm ℤ) (↑x - Catalan.zetaConjInt p K 1) = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range p, x ^ i
Catalan.exists_other_prime_divisor_of_absNorm_gt : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (x : ℤ),
      p < Ideal.absNorm (Ideal.span {↑x - Catalan.zetaConjInt p K 1}) →
        ∃ Q, Q.IsPrime ∧ Q ≠ ⊥ ∧ Q ≠ Catalan.ramifiedPrime p K ∧ ↑x - Catalan.zetaConjInt p K 1 ∈ Q
@Catalan.exists_cyclotomicTraceGaussSum_descended_power : ∀ {F : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [inst : Field F]
  [inst_1 : Fintype F] [inst_2 : Field K] [inst_3 : CharZero K] [inst_4 : Algebra (ZMod (ringChar F)) F] (p : ℕ),
  1 < p →
    p ∣ Fintype.card F - 1 →
      ∀ {μ : K},
        IsPrimitiveRoot μ p →
          ∃ χ,
            orderOf χ = p ∧
              Catalan.cyclotomicTraceGaussSum χ ≠ 0 ∧
                ∃ Γ, (algebraMap K (CyclotomicField (ringChar F) K)) ↑Γ = Catalan.cyclotomicTraceGaussSum χ ^ p
@Catalan.exists_traceGaussSum_quotient_power : ∀ {F : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {E : Type u_3} [inst : Field F]
  [inst_1 : Fintype F] [inst_2 : Field K] [inst_3 : Field E] [inst_4 : Algebra K E]
  [inst_5 : Algebra (ZMod (ringChar F)) F] [CharZero E] [IsGalois K E] [FiniteDimensional K E] (χ : MulChar F K) {p : ℕ}
  [NeZero p],
  χ ^ p = 1 →
    ∀ {μ : K},
      IsPrimitiveRoot μ p →
        ∀ {ζ : E} (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ (ringChar F)) (σ : K ≃+* K) (s : E ≃+* E) (k : ℕ),
          σ μ = μ ^ k →
            s ζ = ζ →
              (∀ (x : K), s ((algebraMap K E) x) = (algebraMap K E) (σ x)) →
                ∀ (Γ : K),
                  Catalan.traceGaussSum χ ⋯ ^ p = (algebraMap K E) Γ →
                    ∃ δ,
                      (algebraMap K E) ↑δ = Catalan.traceGaussSum χ ⋯ ^ k / s (Catalan.traceGaussSum χ ⋯) ∧
                        ↑δ ^ p = Γ ^ k / σ Γ
Catalan.Stickelberger.enatValuation_eq_digitSum : ∀ (v : ℕ → ℕ∞) (b f : ℕ),
  1 < b →
    v 0 = 0 →
      v 1 ≤ 1 →
        (∀ (a c : ℕ), v (a + c) ≤ v a + v c) →
          (∀ (a : ℕ), v (b * a) = v a) →
            (∀ (a : ℕ), 0 < a → a < b ^ f - 1 → v a + v (b ^ f - 1 - a) = ↑(f * (b - 1))) →
              ∀ n < b ^ f - 1, v n = ↑(b.digits n).sum

  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],

Catalan.exists_inverse_normalized_primeResidueGaussSum : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)

    ∃ χ,

        (∀ (x : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K ⧸ I)ˣ),

Catalan.Stickelberger.exists_normalized_residueChar : ∀ (F : Type u_1) [inst : Field F] [inst_1 : Fintype F]
  (R : Type u_2) [inst_2 : CommRing R] [IsDomain R] (n : ℕ) [NeZero n],
  n ∣ Fintype.card F - 1 →
    ∀ (f : R →+* F) {μ : R},
      IsPrimitiveRoot μ n →
        IsPrimitiveRoot (f μ) n →
          ∃ χ, χ ^ n = 1 ∧ (∀ (x : Fˣ), χ ↑x ^ n = 1) ∧ ∀ (x : Fˣ), f (χ ↑x) = ↑x ^ ((Fintype.card F - 1) / n)

@Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum_emultiplicity_one_of_teichmuller : ∀ {ell : ℕ}
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)] {F : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} [inst_1 : Field F] [inst_2 : Fintype F]
  [inst_3 : Algebra (ZMod ell) F] [inst_4 : CommRing A] [IsDedekindDomain A] (I : Ideal A) [CharP F ell]
  [CharP (A ⧸ I ^ 2) ell] (π : A →+* F),
  Function.Surjective ⇑π →
    (∀ (a : A), π a = 0 ↔ a ∈ I) →
      ∀ (τ : MulChar F A),

Catalan.Stickelberger.emultiplicity_span_natCast_eq : ∀ (ell f m n : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime ell)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) [P.IsPrime]
  [P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {↑ell})] [IsCyclotomicExtension {n} ℚ K],
  2 < ell → 0 < f → m = ell ^ f - 1 → n = ell * m → emultiplicity P (Ideal.span {↑ell}) = ↑(ell - 1)
@Catalan.Stickelberger.gaussFamily_emultiplicity_eq_digitSum : ∀ {ell : ℕ} [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)] {A : Type u_1}
  [inst : CommRing A] [IsDedekindDomain A] (I : Ideal A),
  I.IsPrime →
    I ≠ ⊥ →
      ∀ {F : Type u_2} [inst_2 : Field F] [inst_3 : Fintype F] [inst_4 : Algebra (ZMod ell) F] [CharP F ell]
        (π : A →+* F) (d : ℕ),
        Fintype.card F = ell ^ d →
          2 < ell →
            ∀ (τ : MulChar F A) {ζ : A} (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ ell),
              (∀ (x : F), τ x ^ Fintype.card F = τ x) →
                (∀ (x : F), π (τ x) = x⁻¹) →
                  emultiplicity I (Ideal.span {Catalan.Stickelberger.gaussFamily τ ⋯ 1}) = 1 →
                    emultiplicity I (Ideal.span {↑ell}) = ↑(ell - 1) →
                      ∀ a < ell ^ d - 1,
                        emultiplicity I (Ideal.span {Catalan.Stickelberger.gaussFamily τ ⋯ a}) = ↑(ell.digits a).sum
Catalan.Stickelberger.exists_cyclotomic_gaussFamily_emultiplicity_eq_digitSum : ∀ (ell f m n : ℕ)
  [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {n} ℚ K]
  (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) [inst_3 : P.IsMaximal] [P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {↑ell})]
  [inst_5 : Algebra (ZMod ell) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K ⧸ P)],
  2 < ell →
    0 < f →
      m = ell ^ f - 1 →
        n = ell * m →
          ∃ ζ,
            ∃ (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ ell),
              ∃ τ,
                (∀ (x : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K ⧸ P), (Ideal.Quotient.mk P) (τ x) = x⁻¹) ∧
                  ∀ a < ell ^ f - 1,
                    emultiplicity P (Ideal.span {Catalan.Stickelberger.gaussFamily τ ⋯ a}) = ↑(ell.digits a).sum
@Catalan.Stickelberger.ideal_eq_of_emultiplicity_eq : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] [IsDedekindDomain A]
  {I J : Ideal A}, I ≠ 0 → J ≠ 0 → (∀ (P : Ideal A), P.IsPrime → P ≠ ⊥ → emultiplicity P I = emultiplicity P J) → I = J
@Catalan.Stickelberger.emultiplicity_span_eq_zero_of_notMem : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] [IsDomain A]
  {Γ Γ' u : A} {ell f : ℕ},
  IsUnit u → Γ * Γ' = u * ↑ell ^ f → ∀ (P : Ideal A), P.IsPrime → ↑ell ∉ P → emultiplicity P (Ideal.span {Γ}) = 0
@Catalan.Stickelberger.emultiplicity_prod_pow : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] [IsDedekindDomain A]
  {iota : Type u_2} (s : Finset iota) (Q : iota → Ideal A) (e : iota → ℕ) (P : Ideal A),
  Prime P → emultiplicity P (∏ i ∈ s, Q i ^ e i) = ∑ i ∈ s, ↑(e i) * emultiplicity P (Q i)
@Catalan.Stickelberger.emultiplicity_eq_zero_of_ne : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] [IsDedekindDomain A]
  {P Q : Ideal A}, P.IsPrime → Q.IsPrime → Q ≠ ⊥ → P ≠ Q → emultiplicity P Q = 0
@Catalan.Stickelberger.span_eq_prod_pow_of_emultiplicity : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] [IsDedekindDomain A]
  {iota : Type u_2} (s : Finset iota) (Q : iota → Ideal A) (e : iota → ℕ) {Γ : A},
  Γ ≠ 0 →
    (∀ i ∈ s, (Q i).IsPrime) →
      (∀ i ∈ s, Q i ≠ ⊥) →
        (∀ i ∈ s, ∀ j ∈ s, Q i = Q j → i = j) →
          (∀ i ∈ s, emultiplicity (Q i) (Ideal.span {Γ}) = ↑(e i)) →
            (∀ (P : Ideal A), P.IsPrime → P ≠ ⊥ → (∀ i ∈ s, P ≠ Q i) → emultiplicity P (Ideal.span {Γ}) = 0) →
              Ideal.span {Γ} = ∏ i ∈ s, Q i ^ e i
Catalan.ipow_pθ_eq_prod : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (J : Catalan.FracIdealUnit K),
  Catalan.ipow p K J (Catalan.pθ p K) = ∏ a, (Catalan.idealAct K (Catalan.σ p K a)⁻¹) J ^ ↑(↑a).val
@Catalan.idealUnit_eq_prod_pow : ∀ {K : Type u_1} [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] {iota : Type u_2}
  (s : Finset iota) (Q : iota → Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) (e : iota → ℕ)
  (I : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) (hI : I ≠ ⊥),
  I = ∏ i ∈ s, Q i ^ e i →
    ∀ (J : iota → Catalan.FracIdealUnit K), (∀ i ∈ s, ↑(J i) = ↑(Q i)) → Catalan.idealUnit K I hI = ∏ i ∈ s, J i ^ e i
@Catalan.idealUnit_span_eq_principalIdeal : ∀ {K : Type u_1} [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (γ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (u : Kˣ),
  ↑u = (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) γ →
    ∀ (hspan : Ideal.span {γ} ≠ ⊥), Catalan.idealUnit K (Ideal.span {γ}) hspan = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) u
Catalan.conjIdeal_isPrime : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) [P.IsPrime] (a : (ZMod p)ˣ),
  (Catalan.conjIdeal p K P a).IsPrime
Catalan.conjIdeal_ne_bot : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)),
  P ≠ ⊥ → ∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), Catalan.conjIdeal p K P a ≠ ⊥
Catalan.ipow_pθ_eq_principalIdeal_of_emultiplicity : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K))
  [P.IsPrime] (hP0 : P ≠ ⊥) (γ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K),
  γ ≠ 0 →
    ∀ (u : Kˣ),
      ↑u = (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) γ →
        (∀ (a b : (ZMod p)ˣ), Catalan.conjIdeal p K P a = Catalan.conjIdeal p K P b → a = b) →
          (∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), emultiplicity (Catalan.conjIdeal p K P a) (Ideal.span {γ}) = ↑(↑a).val) →
            (∀ (Q : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)),
                Q.IsPrime →
                  Q ≠ ⊥ → (∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), Q ≠ Catalan.conjIdeal p K P a) → emultiplicity Q (Ideal.span {γ}) = 0) →
              Catalan.ipow p K (Catalan.idealUnit K P hP0) (Catalan.pθ p K) = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) u

@Catalan.Stickelberger.span_eq_prod_pow_of_emultiplicity_fiber : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A]
  [IsDedekindDomain A] {iota : Type u_2} (s : Finset iota) (Q : iota → Ideal A) (e : iota → ℕ) {Γ : A},
  Γ ≠ 0 →
    (∀ i ∈ s, (Q i).IsPrime) →
      (∀ i ∈ s, Q i ≠ ⊥) →
        (∀ (P : Ideal A), P.IsPrime → P ≠ ⊥ → emultiplicity P (Ideal.span {Γ}) = ∑ i ∈ s with Q i = P, ↑(e i)) →
          Ideal.span {Γ} = ∏ i ∈ s, Q i ^ e i
Catalan.ipow_pθ_eq_principalIdeal_of_emultiplicity_fiber : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K]
  (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) [P.IsPrime] (hP0 : P ≠ ⊥) (γ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K),
  γ ≠ 0 →
    ∀ (u : Kˣ),
      ↑u = (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) γ →
        (∀ (Q : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)),
            Q.IsPrime → Q ≠ ⊥ → emultiplicity Q (Ideal.span {γ}) = ∑ a with Catalan.conjIdeal p K P a = Q, ↑(↑a).val) →
          Catalan.ipow p K (Catalan.idealUnit K P hP0) (Catalan.pθ p K) = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) u
Catalan.Stickelberger.isCyclotomicExtension_tower : ∀ (N p : ℕ) [NeZero p],
  N ≠ 0 →
    p ∣ N →
      ∀ (L : Type u_1) [inst : Field L] [CharZero L] [inst_2 : Algebra ℚ L] [IsCyclotomicExtension {N} ℚ L],
        IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ ↥(Catalan.Stickelberger.cycloSub p L) ∧
          IsCyclotomicExtension {N} (↥(Catalan.Stickelberger.cycloSub p L)) L
@Catalan.Stickelberger.emultiplicity_map_eq_ramificationIdx_mul : ∀ {A : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [inst : CommRing A]
  [IsDedekindDomain A] [inst_2 : CommRing B] [IsDedekindDomain B] [inst_4 : Algebra A B] (q : Ideal A) (P : Ideal B)
  [q.IsMaximal] [P.IsMaximal] [P.LiesOver q],
  Ideal.map (algebraMap A B) q ≠ ⊥ →
    ∀ (I : Ideal A), I ≠ ⊥ → emultiplicity P (Ideal.map (algebraMap A B) I) = ↑(P.ramificationIdx A) * emultiplicity q I

  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsGalois ℚ K] (ell : ℕ),

      ↑ell ∈ P → ↑ell ∈ Q → ∃ τ, Ideal.map (Catalan.integerAut K τ).toRingHom P = Q
Catalan.Stickelberger.emultiplicity_eq_zero_of_not_conj : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [IsGalois ℚ K] (ell k : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime ell →
    ∀ (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) [P.IsMaximal],
      ↑ell ∈ P →
        ∀ {γ γ' u : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K},

                (∀ (τ : Gal(K/ℚ)), Ideal.map (Catalan.integerAut K τ).toRingHom P ≠ Q) →
                  emultiplicity Q (Ideal.span {γ}) = 0

  {A : Type u_2} [inst : Field F] [inst_1 : Fintype F] [inst_2 : Algebra (ZMod ell) F] [CharP F ell]

    (∀ (x : F), σ (χ x) = χ x) →

@Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum_ringHom_of_fixed_root : ∀ {ell : ℕ} [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)]
  {F : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} [inst_1 : Field F] [inst_2 : Fintype F] [inst_3 : Algebra (ZMod ell) F]
  [inst_4 : CommRing A] (χ : MulChar F A) {ζ : A} (hζ : ζ ^ ell = 1) (σ : A →+* A),
  σ ζ = ζ →
    σ (Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum χ hζ) =
      Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum (χ.ringHomComp σ) hζ

@Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum_pow_mul_inv_pow : ∀ {ell : ℕ} [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)] {F : Type u_1}
  {A : Type u_2} [inst_1 : Field F] [inst_2 : Fintype F] [inst_3 : Algebra (ZMod ell) F] [inst_4 : CommRing A]
  [IsDomain A] (χ : MulChar F A),
  χ ≠ 1 →
    ∀ {ζ : A} (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ ell) (p f : ℕ),
      Fintype.card F = ell ^ f →
        χ (-1) ^ p = 1 →
          Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum χ ⋯ ^ p * Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum χ⁻¹ ⋯ ^ p =
            ↑ell ^ (f * p)
@Catalan.Stickelberger.ringHom_apply_eq_pow_of_pow_eq_one : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] [IsDomain A] {p : ℕ}
  [NeZero p] {ζ : A}, IsPrimitiveRoot ζ p → ∀ (σ : A →+* A) {b : ℕ}, σ ζ = ζ ^ b → ∀ {x : A}, x ^ p = 1 → σ x = x ^ b
@Catalan.Stickelberger.ringHomComp_eq_pow_of_pow_eq_one : ∀ {F : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} [inst : Field F] [Fintype F]
  [inst_2 : CommRing A] [IsDomain A] {p : ℕ} [NeZero p] {ζ : A},
  IsPrimitiveRoot ζ p →
    ∀ (χ : MulChar F A), (∀ (u : Fˣ), χ ↑u ^ p = 1) → ∀ (σ : A →+* A) {b : ℕ}, σ ζ = ζ ^ b → χ.ringHomComp σ = χ ^ b
@Catalan.Stickelberger.enat_eq_natCast_of_natCast_mul_eq : ∀ {e k : ℕ}, e ≠ 0 → ∀ {x : ℕ∞}, ↑e * x = ↑(e * k) → x = ↑k
@Catalan.Stickelberger.emultiplicity_eq_orbit_sum : ∀ {A : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [inst : CommRing A]
  [IsDedekindDomain A] [inst_2 : CommRing B] [IsDedekindDomain B] [inst_4 : Algebra A B] (q : Ideal A) (P : Ideal B)
  [q.IsMaximal] [P.IsMaximal] [P.LiesOver q],
  Ideal.map (algebraMap A B) q ≠ ⊥ →
    ∀ (ell p f d b : ℕ),
      1 < ell →
        1 < p →
          0 < f →
            d * p = ell ^ f - 1 →
              b < p →
                P.ramificationIdx A = ell - 1 →
                  ∀ (γ : A),
                    Ideal.span {γ} ≠ ⊥ →
                      emultiplicity P (Ideal.map (algebraMap A B) (Ideal.span {γ})) = ↑(p * (ell.digits (d * b)).sum) →
                        emultiplicity q (Ideal.span {γ}) = ↑(∑ i ∈ Finset.range f, b * ell ^ i % p)

@Catalan.Stickelberger.eq_of_map_eq_of_pow_eq_one : ∀ {A : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [inst : CommRing A] [IsDomain A]
  [inst_2 : CommRing B] [IsDomain B] {p : ℕ} [NeZero p] (π : A →+* B) {μ : A},
  IsPrimitiveRoot μ p → IsPrimitiveRoot (π μ) p → ∀ {x y : A}, x ^ p = 1 → y ^ p = 1 → π x = π y → x = y
Catalan.Stickelberger.exists_baseChar_eq_invTeichmuller_pow : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  (L : Type u_2) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : Field L] [NumberField L] [inst_4 : Algebra K L]
  [inst_5 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] {F : Type u_3} [inst_6 : Field F] [inst_7 : Fintype F]
  (π : NumberField.RingOfIntegers L →+* F),
  p ∣ Fintype.card F - 1 →
    IsPrimitiveRoot (π ((algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) ⋯.toInteger)) p →
      ∀ (τ : MulChar F (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)),
        (∀ (x : F), π (τ x) = x⁻¹) →
          ∃ χ,
            orderOf χ = p ∧
              χ.ringHomComp (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) =
                τ ^ ((Fintype.card F - 1) / p)
Catalan.exists_cyclotomic_tower_lift : ∀ (p ell m N : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [NeZero ell] [NeZero m]
  (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst_3 : Field K] [inst_4 : NumberField K] [inst_5 : Field L] [inst_6 : NumberField L]
  [inst_7 : Algebra K L] [IsScalarTower ℚ K L] [inst_9 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {N} ℚ L],
  N = ell * m →
    ell.Coprime m →
      p ∣ m →
        ∀ {z : L},
          IsPrimitiveRoot z ell →
            ∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), ∃ s, s z = z ∧ ∀ (x : K), s ((algebraMap K L) x) = (algebraMap K L) ((Catalan.σ p K a) x)
Catalan.conjIdeal_fiber_sum_eq_orbit_sum : ∀ (p ell : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [Fact (Nat.Prime ell)]
  (K : Type u_1) [inst_2 : Field K] [inst_3 : NumberField K] [inst_4 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K]
  (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) [P.IsMaximal] [P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {↑ell})],
  ell.Coprime p →
    ∀ (b : (ZMod p)ˣ),
      ∑ a with Catalan.conjIdeal p K P a = Catalan.conjIdeal p K P b, (↑a).val =
        ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (orderOf ↑ell), (↑b).val * ell ^ i % p
Catalan.Stickelberger.exists_descended_gaussSum_mul_eq : ∀ (p ell f : ℕ) [NeZero p] [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)]
  (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : Field L] [inst_4 : NumberField L]
  [inst_5 : Algebra K L] [IsScalarTower ℚ K L] [IsGalois K L] {ζK : K},
  IsPrimitiveRoot ζK p →
    ∀ {F : Type u_3} [inst_8 : Field F] [inst_9 : Fintype F] [inst_10 : Algebra (ZMod ell) F] [CharP F ell]
      (χ : MulChar F (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)),
      χ ≠ 1 →
        ∀ {ζ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers L} (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ ell),
          Fintype.card F = ell ^ f →
            (∀ (x : F), x ≠ 0 → χ x ^ p = 1) →
              ∀ (γ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K),
                (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) γ =
                    Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum χ ⋯ ^ p →
                  ∃ γ', γ * γ' = ↑ell ^ (f * p)
Catalan.Stickelberger.descended_gaussSum_emultiplicity_eq_zero_of_not_conj : ∀ (p ell f : ℕ) [NeZero p]
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)] (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : Field L] [inst_4 : NumberField L] [inst_5 : Algebra K L] [IsScalarTower ℚ K L] [IsGalois K L] [IsGalois ℚ K]
  {ζK : K},
  IsPrimitiveRoot ζK p →
    ∀ {F : Type u_3} [inst_9 : Field F] [inst_10 : Fintype F] [inst_11 : Algebra (ZMod ell) F] [CharP F ell]
      (χ : MulChar F (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)),
      χ ≠ 1 →
        ∀ {ζ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers L} (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ ell),
          Fintype.card F = ell ^ f →
            (∀ (x : F), x ≠ 0 → χ x ^ p = 1) →
              ∀ (γ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K),
                (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) γ =
                    Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum χ ⋯ ^ p →
                  ∀ (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) [P.IsMaximal],
                    ↑ell ∈ P →
                      ∀ (Q : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) [Q.IsMaximal],
                        (∀ (τ : Gal(K/ℚ)), Ideal.map (Catalan.integerAut K τ).toRingHom P ≠ Q) →
                          emultiplicity Q (Ideal.span {γ}) = 0
Catalan.Stickelberger.emultiplicity_conjIdeal_eq_orbit_sum_of_gaussFamily : ∀ (p ell f m N : ℕ)
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [inst_1 : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)] (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst_2 : Field K]
  [inst_3 : NumberField K] [inst_4 : Field L] [inst_5 : NumberField L] [inst_6 : Algebra K L] [IsScalarTower ℚ K L]
  [inst_8 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {N} ℚ L] (q : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K))
  [q.IsMaximal] [q.LiesOver (Ideal.span {↑ell})] (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) [P.IsMaximal]
  [P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {↑ell})] [P.LiesOver q],
  2 < ell →
    ell ≠ p →
      0 < f →
        m = ell ^ f - 1 →
          N = ell * m →
            p ∣ m →
              ∀ {F : Type u_3} [inst_15 : Field F] [inst_16 : Fintype F] [inst_17 : Algebra (ZMod ell) F] [CharP F ell]
                (χ : MulChar F (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)),
                χ ^ p = 1 →
                  ∀ (τ : MulChar F (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)),
                    χ.ringHomComp (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) =
                        τ ^ (m / p) →
                      ∀ {z : NumberField.RingOfIntegers L} (hz : IsPrimitiveRoot z ell),
                        (∀ a < ell ^ f - 1,
                            emultiplicity P (Ideal.span {Catalan.Stickelberger.gaussFamily τ ⋯ a}) =
                              ↑(ell.digits a).sum) →
                          ∀ (γ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K),
                            γ ≠ 0 →
                              (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) γ =
                                  Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum
                                      (χ.ringHomComp
                                        (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)))
                                      ⋯ ^
                                    p →
                                ∀ (b : (ZMod p)ˣ),
                                  emultiplicity (Catalan.conjIdeal p K q b) (Ideal.span {γ}) =
                                    ↑(∑ i ∈ Finset.range f, (↑b).val * ell ^ i % p)
Catalan.Stickelberger.exists_integralGaussSum_quotient_power : ∀ (p ell m N : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)]
  [inst_1 : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)] [NeZero m] (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst_3 : Field K] [inst_4 : NumberField K]
  [inst_5 : Field L] [inst_6 : NumberField L] [inst_7 : Algebra K L] [IsScalarTower ℚ K L] [IsGalois K L]
  [inst_10 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {N} ℚ L],
  N = ell * m →
    ell.Coprime m →
      p ∣ m →
        ∀ {F : Type u_3} [inst_12 : Field F] [inst_13 : Fintype F] [inst_14 : Algebra (ZMod ell) F] [CharP F ell]
          (χ : MulChar F (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)),
          χ ^ p = 1 →
            ∀ {z : NumberField.RingOfIntegers L} (hz : IsPrimitiveRoot z ell) (γ : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K),
              (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) γ =
                  Catalan.Stickelberger.integralTraceGaussSum
                      (χ.ringHomComp (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L))) ⋯ ^
                    p →
                ∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), ∃ δ, ↑δ ^ p = ↑γ ^ (↑a).val / (Catalan.σ p K a) ↑γ
Catalan.Stickelberger.isCyclotomicExtension_base_tower_of_dvd : ∀ (p N : ℕ) [NeZero p],
  N ≠ 0 →
    p ∣ N →
      ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
        IsCyclotomicExtension {N} ℚ (CyclotomicField N K)
Catalan.Stickelberger.exists_prime_gauss_witnesses : ∀ (p ell : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [hl : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)]
  (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K]
  (q : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) [q.IsMaximal] [q.LiesOver (Ideal.span {↑ell})],
  2 < ell →
    ell ≠ p →
      ∀ (hq0 : q ≠ ⊥),
        ∃ Γ,
          Catalan.ipow p K (Catalan.idealUnit K q hq0) (Catalan.pθ p K) = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) Γ ∧
            ∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), ∃ δ, δ ^ p = Γ ^ (↑a).val / (Catalan.elementAct K (Catalan.σ p K a)) Γ
Catalan.prime_stickelberger_generator : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (I : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)),
  I.IsPrime →
    ∀ (hI0 : I ≠ ⊥),
      2 * ↑p ∉ I →
        ∀ (k : ℕ),
          0 < k →
            k ≤ p → ∃ δ, Catalan.ipow p K (Catalan.idealUnit K I hI0) (Catalan.ΘS p K k) = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) δ
Catalan.stickelberger_annihilates : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  ∀ Θ ∈ Catalan.stickSpan p K,
    ∀ (J : (FractionalIdeal (nonZeroDivisors (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)) K)ˣ),
      ∃ γ, Catalan.ipow p K J Θ = (toPrincipalIdeal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) γ

Catalan.linearLog : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → (K →+* ℂ) → ℤ → Catalan.R p K → ℂ
Catalan.linearLog_zero : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (φ : K →+* ℂ) (x : ℤ),
  Catalan.linearLog p K φ x 0 = 0
Catalan.linearLog_add : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (φ : K →+* ℂ) (x : ℤ) (Θ Ψ : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.linearLog p K φ x (Θ + Ψ) = Catalan.linearLog p K φ x Θ + Catalan.linearLog p K φ x Ψ
Catalan.linearLog_neg : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (φ : K →+* ℂ) (x : ℤ) (Θ : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.linearLog p K φ x (-Θ) = -Catalan.linearLog p K φ x Θ
Catalan.linearLog_sub : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (φ : K →+* ℂ) (x : ℤ) (Θ Ψ : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.linearLog p K φ x (Θ - Ψ) = Catalan.linearLog p K φ x Θ - Catalan.linearLog p K φ x Ψ
Catalan.norm_conj_zeta_div_int : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (φ : K →+* ℂ) (x : ℤ) (τ : Catalan.G p K),
  ‖φ (τ (Catalan.ζ p K)) / ↑x‖ = 1 / |↑x|
Catalan.conj_zeta_div_int_norm_lt_one : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (φ : K →+* ℂ) (x : ℤ),
  1 < |↑x| → ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K), ‖φ (τ (Catalan.ζ p K)) / ↑x‖ < 1
Catalan.exp_linearLog : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (φ : K →+* ℂ) (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  1 < |↑x| →
    ∀ (Θ : Catalan.R p K),
      Catalan.weight p K Θ = 0 →
        Complex.exp (Catalan.linearLog p K φ x Θ) = φ ↑(Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.xmζ p K x hp2) Θ)

  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (φ : K →+* ℂ) (x : ℤ),
  1 < |↑x| → ∀ (Θ : Catalan.R p K), ‖Catalan.linearLog p K φ x Θ‖ ≤ ↑(Catalan.size p K Θ) / (|↑x| - 1)

  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (φ : K →+* ℂ) (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),

Catalan.augAlpha : (p : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst_1 : Field K] →
        [inst_2 : NumberField K] →
          [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] →
            (q : ℕ) → (x : ℤ) → (hp2 : p ≠ 2) → ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2) → Kˣ
Catalan.alphaC : (p : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst_1 : Field K] →
        [inst_2 : NumberField K] →
          [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] →
            (q : ℕ) → (x : ℤ) → (K →+* ℂ) → (hp2 : p ≠ 2) → ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2) → ℂ
Catalan.alphaC_zero : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q → q ≠ 2 → Catalan.alphaC p K q x φ hp2 0 = 1
Catalan.alphaC_add : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (Θ Ψ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
        Catalan.alphaC p K q x φ hp2 (Θ + Ψ) = Catalan.alphaC p K q x φ hp2 Θ * Catalan.alphaC p K q x φ hp2 Ψ
Catalan.alphaC_pow : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  1 < |↑x| →
    ∀ (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
      Catalan.alphaC p K q x φ hp2 Θ ^ q = Complex.exp (Catalan.linearLog p K φ x ↑Θ)
Catalan.phaseValue : (p : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst_1 : Field K] →
        [inst_2 : NumberField K] →
          [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] →
            (q : ℕ) → (x : ℤ) → (K →+* ℂ) → (hp2 : p ≠ 2) → ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2) → ℂ
Catalan.exp_nat_mul_manual : ∀ (z : ℂ) (n : ℕ), Complex.exp (↑n * z) = Complex.exp z ^ n
Catalan.phaseValue_pow : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ)
  (hp2 : p ≠ 2), 1 < |↑x| → ∀ (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)), Catalan.phaseValue p K q x φ hp2 Θ ^ q = 1
Catalan.xi : (p : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst_1 : Field K] →
        [inst_2 : NumberField K] →
          [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] →
            (q : ℕ) →
              [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
                (x : ℤ) → (K →+* ℂ) → (hp2 : p ≠ 2) → 1 < |↑x| → ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2) → ↥(rootsOfUnity q ℂ)
Catalan.xi_val : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (hx : 1 < |↑x|) (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
  ↑↑(Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Θ) = Catalan.phaseValue p K q x φ hp2 Θ
Catalan.xi_zero : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (hx : 1 < |↑x|), Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx 0 = 1
Catalan.xi_add : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (hx : 1 < |↑x|) (Θ Ψ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
        Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx (Θ + Ψ) = Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Θ * Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Ψ
Catalan.xiHom : (p : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst_1 : Field K] →
        [inst_2 : NumberField K] →
          [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] →
            (q : ℕ) →
              [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
                (x : ℤ) →
                  (K →+* ℂ) →
                    (hp2 : p ≠ 2) →
                      p ≠ q → q ≠ 2 → 1 < |↑x| → ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2) →+ Additive ↥(rootsOfUnity q ℂ)
Catalan.xi_neg : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (hx : 1 < |↑x|) (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
        Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx (-Θ) = (Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Θ)⁻¹
Catalan.xi_sub : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (hx : 1 < |↑x|) (Θ Ψ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
        Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx (Θ - Ψ) = Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Θ / Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Ψ
Catalan.rootsOfUnity_eq_of_log_div_norm_lt : ∀ (q : ℕ) [NeZero q] (a : ℂ),
  a ≠ 0 →
    ∀ (η ξ : ↥(rootsOfUnity q ℂ)),
      ‖Complex.log (a / ↑↑η)‖ < Real.pi / ↑q → ‖Complex.log (a / ↑↑ξ)‖ < Real.pi / ↑q → η = ξ
Catalan.xi_small_log : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (hx : 1 < |↑x|),
  ∀ r < Real.pi / 2 * (|↑x| - 1),
    ∀ (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
      ↑(Catalan.size p K ↑Θ) ≤ 2 * r →
        ‖Complex.log (Catalan.alphaC p K q x φ hp2 Θ / ↑↑(Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Θ))‖ ≤
            ↑(Catalan.size p K ↑Θ) / (↑q * (|↑x| - 1)) ∧
          ↑(Catalan.size p K ↑Θ) / (↑q * (|↑x| - 1)) < Real.pi / ↑q
Catalan.xi_unique : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (hx : 1 < |↑x|),
  ∀ r < Real.pi / 2 * (|↑x| - 1),
    ∀ (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
      ↑(Catalan.size p K ↑Θ) ≤ 2 * r →
        ∀ (η : ↥(rootsOfUnity q ℂ)),
          ‖Complex.log (Catalan.alphaC p K q x φ hp2 Θ / ↑↑η)‖ ≤ ↑(Catalan.size p K ↑Θ) / (↑q * (|↑x| - 1)) →
            η = Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Θ
Catalan.xi_local_add : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (φ : K →+* ℂ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (hx : 1 < |↑x|) (r : ℝ) (Θ Ψ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
        ↑(Catalan.size p K ↑Θ) ≤ r →
          ↑(Catalan.size p K ↑Ψ) ≤ r →
            Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx (Θ + Ψ) = Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Θ * Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Ψ
Catalan.expm1_seven_fifths : ∀ (z : ℂ), ‖z‖ ≤ Real.pi / 5 → ‖Complex.exp z - 1‖ ≤ 7 / 5 * ‖z‖
Catalan.even_degree_power_bound : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)], p ≠ 2 → (↑p - 1) ^ 2 ≤ 2 ^ (p - 1)
Catalan.small_ratio_le : ∀ (d n X ε s : ℝ),
  2 ≤ d → 0 < n → 36 ≤ X → 0 < ε → 0 ≤ s → s ≤ 2 * ((2 - ε) * n / d) → s / (n * (X - 1)) ≤ 2 / 35
Catalan.five_pow_hundred : ¬5 ^ 100 ≡ 1 [MOD 101 ^ 2]
Catalan.reduce_to_primes : (∀ (p q : ℕ),
    Nat.Prime p → Nat.Prime q → ∀ (x y : ℤ), 1 < x → 1 < y → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → p = 2 ∧ q = 3 ∧ x = 3 ∧ y = 2) →
  ∀ (x y : ℤ) (a b : ℕ), 1 < a → 1 < b → 1 < x → 1 < y → x ^ a = y ^ b + 1 → a = 2 ∧ b = 3 ∧ x = 3 ∧ y = 2

Catalan.mihRadius : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ → ℝ
Catalan.mihThreshold : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ
Catalan.h8_consequences : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (x : ℤ) (ε : ℝ),
      0 < ε →
        ε ≤ 1 →
          |↑x| ≥ max (Catalan.mihThreshold p ε) (4 / Real.pi * ↑q / (↑p - 1) + 1) →
            36 ≤ |↑x| ∧
              Catalan.mihRadius p q ε < Real.pi / 2 * (|↑x| - 1) ∧
                Real.log (36 * 2 ^ (p - 1) / (↑p - 1) ^ 2) ≤ ε * Real.log |↑x|
Catalan.prop49_real_contradiction : ∀ (d : ℕ),
  2 ≤ d →
    ∀ (n X ε s H U : ℝ),
      0 < n →
        36 ≤ X →
          0 < ε →
            ε ≤ 1 →
              0 < s →
                s ≤ 2 * ((2 - ε) * n / ↑d) →
                  Real.log (36 * 2 ^ d / ↑d ^ 2) ≤ ε * Real.log X →
                    H ≤ s / (2 * n) * Real.log (X + 1) + Real.log 2 →
                      0 ≤ U → U ≤ 7 / 5 * (s / (n * (X - 1))) → Real.exp (-(↑d * H)) ≤ U ^ 2 → False
Catalan.root_phase_separation : ∀ (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  q ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (a : ℂ),
      a ≠ 0 → ∀ (η : ↥(rootsOfUnity q ℂ)), η ≠ 1 → ‖Complex.log (a / ↑↑η)‖ < Real.pi / ↑q → 5 / 2 / ↑q < ‖a - 1‖
Catalan.prop42_real_contradiction : ∀ (d n P X H : ℝ),
  0 < d →
    0 < n →
      0 < P →
        0 < X →
          H < Real.log (0.8 * n) + Real.log P / d →
            Real.log X ≤ n * H + 3 * Real.log 2 → 8 * (0.8 * n * P ^ (1 / d)) ^ n ≤ X → False
Catalan.cor43_power_domination : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 → p < q → 36 * 2 ^ (p - 1) / (↑p - 1) ^ 2 ≤ 8 * (0.8 * ↑q) ^ q
Catalan.pPrime : ℕ → ℤ → ℕ
Catalan.pPrime_pos : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (x : ℤ), 0 < Catalan.pPrime p x
Catalan.radius_ge_one : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)],
  p ≠ 2 → ∀ (ε : ℝ), ↑p ≤ (2 - ε) * ↑q + 1 → 1 ≤ Catalan.mihRadius p q ε
Catalan.h9_implies_h8 : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ),
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (ε : ℝ),
        0 < ε →
          ε ≤ 1 →
            |↑x| ≥ max (Catalan.mihThreshold p ε) (8 * (0.8 * ↑q * ↑(Catalan.pPrime p x) ^ (1 / (↑p - 1))) ^ q) →
              |↑x| ≥ max (Catalan.mihThreshold p ε) (4 / Real.pi * ↑q / (↑p - 1) + 1)

Catalan.finitePlace_integral_le_one : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K) (a : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K), v ↑a ≤ 1
Catalan.finitePlace_le_of_dvd : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K) (a b : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K), a ∣ b → v ↑b ≤ v ↑a
Catalan.finitePlace_eq_of_span_eq : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K) (a b : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K), Ideal.span {a} = Ideal.span {b} → v ↑a = v ↑b
Catalan.finitePlace_root_distance_pow : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K) (τ : Catalan.G p K),
  v (1 - τ (Catalan.ζ p K)) ^ (p - 1) = v ↑p
Catalan.finitePlace_root_difference_pow : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K) (σ τ : Catalan.G p K),
      σ ≠ τ → v (τ (Catalan.ζ p K) - σ (Catalan.ζ p K)) ^ (p - 1) = v ↑p
Catalan.prop42_power_difference : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)) (σ τ : Catalan.G p K),
  ↑Θ = MonoidAlgebra.single σ 1 - MonoidAlgebra.single τ 1 →
    ↑(Catalan.augAlpha p K q x hp2 Θ) ^ q - 1 = (τ (Catalan.ζ p K) - σ (Catalan.ζ p K)) / (↑x - τ (Catalan.ζ p K))
Catalan.finite_power_near_one : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (q : ℕ)
  (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K) (a : K), v (a - 1) < 1 → v (a ^ q - 1) ≤ v (a - 1)
Catalan.finite_log_inverse_sum_le_of_lower : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (m : ℕ),
  0 < m →
    ∀ (r : ℝ),
      0 ≤ r →
        ∀ (a : K),
          a ≠ 0 →
            (∀ (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K), v ↑m ^ r ≤ v a) →
              ∑ᶠ (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K), Real.log (max (v a⁻¹) 1) ≤ r * ↑(Module.finrank ℚ K) * Real.log ↑m
Catalan.prop42_finite_lower : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
  Catalan.size p K ↑Θ ≤ 2 →
    ↑Θ ≠ 0 →
      ∀ (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K),
        v ↑(Catalan.pPrime p x) ^ (1 / (↑p - 1)) ≤ v (↑(Catalan.augAlpha p K q x hp2 Θ) - 1)
Catalan.finite_inverse_sum_bound_for_cyclotomic : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (m : ℕ),
  0 < m →
    ∀ (a : K),
      a ≠ 0 →
        (∀ (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K), v ↑m ^ (1 / (↑p - 1)) ≤ v a) →
          ∑ᶠ (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K), Real.log (max (v a⁻¹) 1) ≤ Real.log ↑m
Catalan.augAlpha_sub_one_ne_zero_of_size_two : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)), Catalan.size p K ↑Θ ≤ 2 → ↑Θ ≠ 0 → ↑(Catalan.augAlpha p K q x hp2 Θ) - 1 ≠ 0
Catalan.prop42_nonarch_ge_one : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  x ≡ 1 [ZMOD ↑p] →
    ∀ (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
      Catalan.size p K ↑Θ ≤ 2 →
        ↑Θ ≠ 0 → ∀ (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K), 1 ≤ v (↑(Catalan.augAlpha p K q x hp2 Θ) - 1)
Catalan.prop42_finite_inverse_sum : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
  Catalan.size p K ↑Θ ≤ 2 →
    ↑Θ ≠ 0 →
      ∑ᶠ (v : NumberField.FinitePlace K), Real.log (max (v (↑(Catalan.augAlpha p K q x hp2 Θ) - 1)⁻¹) 1) ≤
        Real.log ↑(Catalan.pPrime p x)

Catalan.A1e.upow_base_div : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (a b : Kˣ)
  (T : Catalan.R p K), Catalan.upow p K (a / b) T = Catalan.upow p K a T / Catalan.upow p K b T
Catalan.A1e.actUnit_one : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (a : Kˣ),
  (Catalan.actUnit p K 1) a = a
Catalan.A1e.upow_actUnit_commute : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (a : Kˣ) (T : Catalan.R p K) (τ : Catalan.G p K),
  Catalan.upow p K ((Catalan.actUnit p K τ) a) T = (Catalan.actUnit p K τ) (Catalan.upow p K a T)
Catalan.A1e.upow_minusPart_eq : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (a : Kˣ) (T : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.upow p K a (Catalan.A1e.minusPart p K T) =
    Catalan.upow p K a T / (Catalan.actUnit p K (Catalan.ι p K)) (Catalan.upow p K a T)
Catalan.A1e.upow_minusPart_base : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (a : Kˣ) (T : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.upow p K a (Catalan.A1e.minusPart p K T) = Catalan.upow p K (a / (Catalan.actUnit p K (Catalan.ι p K)) a) T
Catalan.A1e.upow_pow_eq_one : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (a : Kˣ)
  (T : Catalan.R p K) (n : ℕ), a ^ n = 1 → Catalan.upow p K a T ^ n = 1
Catalan.A1e.iota_apply_zeta : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K], (Catalan.ι p K) (Catalan.ζ p K) = (Catalan.ζ p K)⁻¹
Catalan.A1e.minus_root_quotient : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (d : Kˣ),
  ↑d = 1 - Catalan.ζ p K → ↑(d / (Catalan.actUnit p K (Catalan.ι p K)) d) = -Catalan.ζ p K
Catalan.A1e.solution_primes_ne : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → p ≠ q
Catalan.A1e.lift_minus_one : ∀ (q : ℕ), Odd q → ∀ (a : ℤ), a ≡ -1 [ZMOD ↑q] → a ^ q ≡ -1 [ZMOD ↑q ^ 2]
Catalan.A1e.wieferich_of_factorization : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q → q ≠ 2 → p ≠ q → ∀ (x a : ℤ), ↑q ^ 2 ∣ x → x - 1 = ↑p ^ (q - 1) * a ^ q → p ^ (q - 1) ≡ 1 [MOD q ^ 2]
Catalan.A1e.inverseConjugates_basis : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  ∃ B, ∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), B a = Catalan.A1e.inverseConjugate p K a
Catalan.A1e.inverseConjugates_dvd_coeff : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (n : ℤ) (c : (ZMod p)ˣ → ℤ),
  ↑n ∣ ∑ a, ↑(c a) * Catalan.A1e.inverseConjugate p K a → ∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), n ∣ c a
Catalan.A1e.testTheta_coefficient : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 → ∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), (Catalan.A1e.testTheta p K).coeff (Catalan.σ p K a)⁻¹ = if 2 * (↑a).val < p then -1 else 1
Catalan.A1e.iota_on_embeddings : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 → ∀ (φ : K →+* ℂ) (a : K), φ ((Catalan.ι p K) a) = (starRingEnd ℂ) (φ a)
Catalan.A1e.torsion_order_bound : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 → ∀ (t : Kˣ), (∃ n, 0 < n ∧ t ^ n = 1) → t ^ (2 * p) = 1
Catalan.A1e.symmetry : ∀ (p q : ℕ), Odd p → Odd q → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → (-y) ^ q = (-x) ^ p + 1
@Catalan.A1e.one_add_pow_remainder : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] (a : A) (n : ℕ),
  ∃ r, (1 + a) ^ n = 1 + ↑n * a + a ^ 2 * r
@Catalan.A1e.one_add_mul_pow_dvd : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] (q : ℕ) (t : A), ↑q ^ 2 ∣ (1 + ↑q * t) ^ q - 1
@Catalan.A1e.one_sub_pow_remainder : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] (t z : A) (n : ℕ),
  ∃ r, (1 - t * z) ^ n = 1 - t * (↑n * z) + t ^ 2 * r
@Catalan.A1e.prod_one_sub_remainder : ∀ {A : Type u_1} {I : Type u_2} [inst : CommRing A] (s : Finset I) (t : A)
  (z : I → A) (m : I → ℕ), ∃ r, ∏ i ∈ s, (1 - t * z i) ^ m i = 1 - t * ∑ i ∈ s, ↑(m i) * z i + t ^ 2 * r
@Catalan.A1e.primary_of_frobenius_lift : ∀ {A : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing A] (q : ℕ) (F : A ≃+* A),
  (∀ (a : A), ↑q ∣ a ^ q - F a) → ∀ (b : A), ↑q ∣ b ^ q - 1 → ↑q ^ 2 ∣ b ^ q - 1
@Catalan.A1e.integral_qth_root : ∀ {K : Type u_1} [inst : Field K] [NumberField K] (q : ℕ),
  0 < q → ∀ (A : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (b : K), b ^ q = ↑A → ∃ B, B ^ q = A
@Catalan.A1e.kronecker_unit_pair : ∀ {K : Type u_1} [inst : Field K] [NumberField K]
  (u v : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
  (∀ (φ : K →+* ℂ),
      φ ((algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) ↑v) =
        (starRingEnd ℂ) (φ ((algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) ↑u))) →
    ∃ n,
      0 < n ∧
        ((algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) ↑u / (algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) ↑v) ^ n = 1
@Catalan.A1e.qth_root_of_bezout : ∀ {M : Type u_1} [inst : CommGroup M] (z : M) (N q : ℕ) (r s : ℤ),
  z ^ N = 1 → r * ↑q + s * ↑N = 1 → ∃ w, w ^ q = z
@Catalan.A1e.qth_root_of_coprime : ∀ {M : Type u_1} [inst : CommGroup M] (z : M) (N q : ℕ),
  z ^ N = 1 → q.Coprime N → ∃ w, w ^ q = z

  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Catalan.R p K → Catalan.R p K

  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) → [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Catalan.R p K
Catalan.A1e.lambdaUnit : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) → [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → ℤ → p ≠ 2 → Kˣ
Catalan.A1e.zetaInteger : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → NumberField.RingOfIntegers K

  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst_1 : Field K] →

Catalan.A1e.liftCoefficient : (p : ℕ) → [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] → ℕ → (ZMod p)ˣ → ℕ
Catalan.A1e.liftProduct : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → ℕ → ℤ → NumberField.RingOfIntegers K

      [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → ℕ → NumberField.RingOfIntegers K

  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K], Catalan.ΘS p K 2 ∈ Catalan.stickSpan p K
Catalan.A1e.super_cassels_in_field : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → p ≠ q → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → ↑q ^ 2 ∣ x
Catalan.A1e.super_cassels : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → ↑q ^ 2 ∣ x
Catalan.A1e.one_sided_wieferich : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → p ^ (q - 1) ≡ 1 [MOD q ^ 2]
Catalan.double_wieferich : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q →
      p ≠ 2 →
        q ≠ 2 →
          ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → q ^ (p - 1) ≡ 1 [MOD p ^ 2] ∧ p ^ (q - 1) ≡ 1 [MOD q ^ 2]
Catalan.A1e.cyclotomic_frobenius_lift : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → p ≠ q → ∃ F, ∀ (a : NumberField.RingOfIntegers K), ↑q ∣ a ^ q - F a
Catalan.A1e.exists_unit_mul_of_principalIdeal_eq : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (x y : Kˣ),
  (Catalan.principalIdeal K) x = (Catalan.principalIdeal K) y →
    ∃ e, x = (Units.map ↑(algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K)) e * y
Catalan.A1e.lambda_stick_generator : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    ∀ (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
      q ≠ 2 →
        ∀ (x y : ℤ),
          x ≠ 0 →
            y ≠ 0 →
              x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
                ∀ T ∈ Catalan.stickSpan p K,
                  ∃ e a,
                    Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.A1e.lambdaUnit p K x hp2) T =
                      (Units.map ↑(algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K)) e * a ^ q
Catalan.A1e.liftProduct_qth : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    ∀ (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
      q ≠ 2 →
        p ≠ q →
          ∀ (x : ℤ),
            (∃ b, Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.xmζ p K x hp2) (Catalan.A1e.testTheta p K) = b ^ q) →
              ∃ b, b ^ q = ↑(Catalan.A1e.liftProduct p K q x)
Catalan.A1e.kummer_unit_quotient : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
      ∃ n,
        0 < n ∧
          ((algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) ↑u /
                (Catalan.ι p K) ((algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) K) ↑u)) ^
              n =
            1
Catalan.A1e.minus_stick_qth : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    ∀ (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
      q ≠ 2 →
        p ≠ q →
          ∀ (x y : ℤ),
            x ≠ 0 →
              y ≠ 0 →
                x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
                  ∀ T ∈ Catalan.stickSpan p K,
                    ∃ b, Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.xmζ p K x hp2) (Catalan.A1e.minusPart p K T) = b ^ q
Catalan.A1e.liftProduct_expansion : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) (x : ℤ),
  ∃ r, Catalan.A1e.liftProduct p K q x = 1 - ↑x * Catalan.A1e.liftLinear p K q + ↑x ^ 2 * r
Catalan.A1e.liftProduct_mod_q : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) (x : ℤ),
  ↑q ∣ x → ↑q ∣ Catalan.A1e.liftProduct p K q x - 1
Catalan.A1e.liftProduct_obstruction : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    p ≠ q →
      ∀ (x : ℤ), ↑q ∣ x → (∃ b, b ^ q = ↑(Catalan.A1e.liftProduct p K q x)) → ↑q ^ 2 ∣ ↑x * Catalan.A1e.liftLinear p K q

  p ≠ 2 → Catalan.A1e.liftCoefficient p q 1 = q ^ 2 - 1

Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_imag_pow_even : ∀ (v : ℤ) (j : ℕ), { re := 0, im := v } ^ (2 * j) = ↑((-1) ^ j * v ^ (2 * j))
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_imag_pow_odd_re : ∀ (v : ℤ) (j : ℕ), ({ re := 0, im := v } ^ (2 * j + 1)).re = 0
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_imag_pow_re : ∀ (v : ℤ) (k : ℕ),
  ({ re := 0, im := v } ^ k).re = if Even k then (-1) ^ (k / 2) * v ^ k else 0
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_re_binomial : ∀ (v : ℤ) (p : ℕ),
  ({ re := 1, im := v } ^ p).re = ∑ j ∈ Finset.range (p / 2 + 1), (-1) ^ j * ↑(p.choose (2 * j)) * v ^ (2 * j)
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_re_dvd_re_pow_odd : ∀ (z : GaussianInt) (n : ℕ), Odd n → z.re ∣ (z ^ n).re
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_re_pow_mod_four_of_even_im : ∀ (z : GaussianInt),
  Even z.im → ∀ (n : ℕ), (z ^ n).re ≡ z.re ^ n [ZMOD 4]
Catalan.Lebesgue.lebesgue_solution_parity : ∀ (p : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → p ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ^ p = y ^ 2 + 1 → Even y ∧ Odd x
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_conjugate_coprime_of_even : ∀ (y : ℤ),
  Even y → IsCoprime { re := 1, im := y } { re := 1, im := -y }
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_unit_pow_four : ∀ (u : GaussianIntˣ), u ^ 4 = 1
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_unit_pow_surjective : ∀ (n : ℕ), Odd n → ∀ (u : GaussianIntˣ), ∃ v, v ^ n = u
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_root_normalization : ∀ (p : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    p ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (y : ℤ),
        Even y →
          y ≠ 0 → ∀ (z : GaussianInt), z ^ p = { re := 1, im := y } → ∃ v, Even v ∧ v ≠ 0 ∧ z = { re := 1, im := v }
Catalan.Lebesgue.lebesgue_even_term_order : ∀ (p j : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    p ≠ 2 →
      2 ≤ j →
        2 * j ≤ p →
          ∀ (v : ℤ),
            Even v →
              v ≠ 0 → emultiplicity 2 (↑(p.choose 2) * v ^ 2) < emultiplicity 2 (↑(p.choose (2 * j)) * v ^ (2 * j))
@Catalan.Lebesgue.int_sum_ne_zero_of_unique_min_order : ∀ {ι : Type u_1} [DecidableEq ι] (s : Finset ι),
  ∀ i ∈ s, ∀ (f : ι → ℤ), f i ≠ 0 → (∀ j ∈ s, j ≠ i → emultiplicity 2 (f i) < emultiplicity 2 (f j)) → ∑ j ∈ s, f j ≠ 0
Catalan.Lebesgue.gaussian_even_imag_real_ne_one : ∀ (p : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → p ≠ 2 → ∀ (v : ℤ), Even v → v ≠ 0 → ({ re := 1, im := v } ^ p).re ≠ 1
Catalan.Lebesgue.exists_gaussian_root_of_solution : ∀ (p : ℕ),
  Odd p → ∀ (x y : ℤ), Even y → x ^ p = y ^ 2 + 1 → ∃ z, z ^ p = { re := 1, im := y }
Catalan.lebesgue_q_two : ∀ (p : ℕ), Nat.Prime p → p ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p ≠ y ^ 2 + 1

Catalan.Euler.sq_of_nonneg_coprime_product : ∀ (a b c : ℤ),
  0 ≤ a → IsCoprime a b → a * b = c ^ 2 → ∃ r, 0 ≤ r ∧ a = r ^ 2
Catalan.Euler.sq_sub_sq_eq_one : ∀ (a b : ℤ), a ^ 2 - b ^ 2 = 1 → (a = 1 ∨ a = -1) ∧ b = 0
Catalan.Euler.euler_factors : ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ^ 2 = y ^ 3 + 1 → (y + 1) * (y ^ 2 - y + 1) = x ^ 2
Catalan.Euler.euler_y_pos : ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ 2 = y ^ 3 + 1 → 0 < y
Catalan.Euler.sq_eq_quadratic : ∀ (y a : ℤ), 0 ≤ y → 0 ≤ a → y ^ 2 - y + 1 = a ^ 2 → y = 0 ∨ y = 1
Catalan.Euler.euler_three_square_factors : ∀ (x y : ℤ),
  x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ 2 = y ^ 3 + 1 → 3 ∣ y + 1 → ∃ a b, 0 < a ∧ 0 ≤ b ∧ y + 1 = 3 * a ^ 2 ∧ y ^ 2 - y + 1 = 3 * b ^ 2
Catalan.Euler.int_sq_ne_two : ∀ (x : ℤ), x ^ 2 ≠ 2
Catalan.Euler.euler_three_dvd_y_add_one : ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ 2 = y ^ 3 + 1 → 3 ∣ y + 1
Catalan.Euler.pell_complete_int : ∀ (x y : ℤ),
  0 ≤ x → 0 ≤ y → x ^ 2 - 3 * y ^ 2 = 1 → ∃ n, x = ↑(Catalan.Euler.pellX n) ∧ y = ↑(Catalan.Euler.pellY n)
Catalan.Euler.euler_solution_pell : ∀ (x y : ℤ),
  x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ 2 = y ^ 3 + 1 → ∃ a n, y + 1 = 3 * ↑a ^ 2 ∧ Catalan.Euler.pellY n + 1 = 2 * a ^ 2
Catalan.Euler.pellX : ℕ → ℕ
Catalan.Euler.pellY : ℕ → ℕ
Catalan.Euler.negative_pell_two_param : ∀ (x y : ℤ),
  0 ≤ x →
    0 ≤ y →
      2 * x ^ 2 - y ^ 2 = 1 →
        ∃ a b, 0 ≤ a ∧ 0 ≤ b ∧ a ^ 2 - 2 * b ^ 2 = 1 ∧ (x = 2 * a ^ 2 - 1 + 2 * a * b ∨ x = 2 * a ^ 2 - 1 - 2 * a * b)
Catalan.Euler.quartic_pell_three : ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ^ 4 - 3 * y ^ 2 = 1 → (x = 1 ∨ x = -1) ∧ y = 0
Catalan.Euler.quartic_pell_two : ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ^ 4 - 2 * y ^ 2 = 1 → (x = 1 ∨ x = -1) ∧ y = 0
Catalan.Euler.pell_identity : ∀ (n : ℕ), ↑(Catalan.Euler.pellX n) ^ 2 - 3 * ↑(Catalan.Euler.pellY n) ^ 2 = 1
Catalan.Euler.pell_complete : ∀ (x y : ℕ),
  ↑x ^ 2 - 3 * ↑y ^ 2 = 1 → ∃ n, x = Catalan.Euler.pellX n ∧ y = Catalan.Euler.pellY n
Catalan.Euler.pell_x_double : ∀ (n : ℕ), Catalan.Euler.pellX (2 * n) + 1 = 2 * Catalan.Euler.pellX n ^ 2
Catalan.Euler.pell_y_double : ∀ (n : ℕ), Catalan.Euler.pellY (2 * n) = 2 * Catalan.Euler.pellX n * Catalan.Euler.pellY n
Catalan.Euler.pell_x_odd : ∀ (n : ℕ),
  Catalan.Euler.pellX (2 * n + 1) = (Catalan.Euler.pellY n + Catalan.Euler.pellY (n + 1)) ^ 2 + 1
Catalan.Euler.pell_y_odd : ∀ (n : ℕ),
  Catalan.Euler.pellY (2 * n + 1) + 1 = 2 * Catalan.Euler.pellX n * Catalan.Euler.pellY (n + 1)
Catalan.Euler.pell_x_odd_iff : ∀ (n : ℕ), Odd (Catalan.Euler.pellX n) ↔ Even n
Catalan.Euler.pell_y_odd_iff : ∀ (n : ℕ), Odd (Catalan.Euler.pellY n) ↔ Odd n
Catalan.Euler.pell_x_eq_one_iff : ∀ (n : ℕ), Catalan.Euler.pellX n = 1 ↔ n = 0
Catalan.Euler.pell_y_pos : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < n → 0 < Catalan.Euler.pellY n
Catalan.Euler.pell_xy_coprime : ∀ (n : ℕ), (Catalan.Euler.pellX n).Coprime (Catalan.Euler.pellY n)
Catalan.Euler.pell_y_plus_one_twice_square_of_x_square_trivial : (∀ (n a : ℕ), Catalan.Euler.pellX n = a ^ 2 → n = 0) →
  ∀ (n a : ℕ), Catalan.Euler.pellY n + 1 = 2 * a ^ 2 → n = 1
Catalan.Euler.pell_x_square_index_zero : ∀ (n a : ℕ), Catalan.Euler.pellX n = a ^ 2 → n = 0
Catalan.Euler.pell_y_plus_one_twice_square : ∀ (n a : ℕ), Catalan.Euler.pellY n + 1 = 2 * a ^ 2 → n = 1
Catalan.euler_square_cube : ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ 2 = y ^ 3 + 1 → (x = 3 ∨ x = -3) ∧ y = 2

Catalan.KoChao.square_base_oriented_factors : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ),
        x ≠ 0 →
          y ≠ 0 →
            Odd x →
              x ^ 2 = y ^ q + 1 →
                ∃ z a b,
                  (z = x ∨ z = -x) ∧
                    a ≠ 0 ∧ b ≠ 0 ∧ Odd b ∧ z - 1 = 2 ^ (q - 1) * a ^ q ∧ z + 1 = 2 * b ^ q ∧ IsCoprime (b ^ 2) (2 * a)
Catalan.KoChao.gcd_sub_homogeneous_sum : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    ∀ (A B : ℤ),
      IsCoprime A B →
        (A - B).gcd (∑ i ∈ Finset.range q, A ^ i * B ^ (q - 1 - i)) = 1 ∨
          (A - B).gcd (∑ i ∈ Finset.range q, A ^ i * B ^ (q - 1 - i)) = q
Catalan.KoChao.integer_square_gap : ∀ (b c : ℤ), b ^ 2 ≠ c ^ 2 → 2 * |b| - 1 ≤ |b ^ 2 - c ^ 2|
Catalan.KoChao.oriented_factor_abs_lt : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  5 ≤ q → ∀ (z a b : ℤ), b ≠ 0 → z - 1 = 2 ^ (q - 1) * a ^ q → z + 1 = 2 * b ^ q → |a| < |b|
Catalan.KoChao.oriented_difference_not_square : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  5 ≤ q → ∀ (z a b : ℤ), a ≠ 0 → b ≠ 0 → z - 1 = 2 ^ (q - 1) * a ^ q → z + 1 = 2 * b ^ q → ¬∃ c, b ^ 2 - 2 * a = c ^ 2
Catalan.KoChao.oriented_square_identity : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  0 < q → ∀ (z a b : ℤ), z - 1 = 2 ^ (q - 1) * a ^ q → z + 1 = 2 * b ^ q → (b ^ 2) ^ q - (2 * a) ^ q = (b ^ q - 2) ^ 2
Catalan.KoChao.square_base_positive_even : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ 2 = y ^ q + 1 → 0 < y ∧ Even y ∧ Odd x
Catalan.KoChao.square_base_q_dvd_x : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ 2 = y ^ q + 1 → ↑q ∣ x
Catalan.KoChao.prime_dvd_or_square_of_pow_sub_pow_eq_sq : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (A B C : ℤ), IsCoprime A B → A ^ q - B ^ q = C ^ 2 → ↑q ∣ C ∨ ∃ r, A - B = r ^ 2
Catalan.KoChao.oriented_second_congruence : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    5 ≤ q →
      ∀ (z a b : ℤ),
        a ≠ 0 → b ≠ 0 → z - 1 = 2 ^ (q - 1) * a ^ q → z + 1 = 2 * b ^ q → IsCoprime (b ^ 2) (2 * a) → ↑q ∣ z - 3
Catalan.KoChao.prime_eq_three_of_two_congruences : ∀ (q : ℕ), Nat.Prime q → ∀ (z : ℤ), ↑q ∣ z → ↑q ∣ z - 3 → q = 3
Catalan.koChao_p_two : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ 2 = y ^ q + 1 → (x = 3 ∨ x = -3) ∧ y = 2 ∧ q = 3

Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ
Catalan.LatticeCount.finite_lattice_ball : ∀ (n r : ℕ), {c | ∑ i, |c i| ≤ ↑r}.Finite
Catalan.LatticeCount.S_zero_dim : ∀ (r : ℕ), Catalan.S 0 r = 1
Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial_two : ∀ (r : ℕ), Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial 2 r = 2 * r ^ 2 + 2 * r + 1
Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial_three : ∀ (r : ℕ),
  3 * Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial 3 r = 4 * r ^ 3 + 6 * r ^ 2 + 8 * r + 3
Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial_gt_two : ∀ (r : ℕ),
  9 ≤ r → 4 * 2 ^ 2 * (r + 1) < Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial 2 r
Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial_gt_three : ∀ (r : ℕ),
  5 ≤ r → 4 * 3 ^ 2 * (r + 1) < Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial 3 r
Catalan.LatticeCount.S_succ_dim : ∀ (n r : ℕ),
  Catalan.S (n + 1) r = Catalan.S n r + 2 * ∑ t ∈ Finset.range r, Catalan.S n t
Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial_zero_dim : ∀ (r : ℕ), Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial 0 r = 1
Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial_succ_dim : ∀ (n r : ℕ),
  Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial (n + 1) r =
    Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial n r + 2 * ∑ t ∈ Finset.range r, Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial n t
Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial_gt : ∀ (n r : ℕ),
  11 ≤ n → 3 ≤ r → 4 * n ^ 2 * (r + 1) < Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial n r
Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial_gt' : ∀ (n r : ℕ),
  5 ≤ n → 4 ≤ r → 4 * n ^ 2 * (r + 1) < Catalan.LatticeCount.countPolynomial n r
Catalan.S_formula : ∀ (n r : ℕ), Catalan.S n r = ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), 2 ^ k * n.choose k * r.choose k
Catalan.S_gt : ∀ (n r : ℕ), 11 ≤ n → 3 ≤ r → 4 * n ^ 2 * (r + 1) < Catalan.S n r
Catalan.S_gt' : ∀ (n r : ℕ), 5 ≤ n → 4 ≤ r → 4 * n ^ 2 * (r + 1) < Catalan.S n r
Catalan.S_gt_three : ∀ (r : ℕ), 5 ≤ r → 4 * 3 ^ 2 * (r + 1) < Catalan.S 3 r
Catalan.S_gt_two : ∀ (r : ℕ), 9 ≤ r → 4 * 2 ^ 2 * (r + 1) < Catalan.S 2 r

Catalan.small_five_wieferich_candidates : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q ≠ 2 → q ≠ 5 → q < 144 → q ^ 4 ≡ 1 [MOD 25] → q = 7 ∨ q = 43 ∨ q = 101 ∨ q = 107
Catalan.five_no_double_wieferich_small : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q ≠ 2 → q ≠ 5 → q < 144 → ¬(q ^ 4 ≡ 1 [MOD 25] ∧ 5 ^ (q - 1) ≡ 1 [MOD q ^ 2])
Catalan.mignotte_lower_bound : ∀ (p q : ℕ), Nat.Prime p → Nat.Prime q → 5 ≤ p → q ≡ 1 [MOD p ^ 2] → 4 * p ^ 2 + 1 ≤ q
Catalan.CaseTwo.not_modEq_one_of_large_weak_bound : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → Nat.Prime q → 5 ≤ p → q < 4 * (p - 1) ^ 2 → q ^ (p - 1) ≡ 1 [MOD p ^ 2] → ¬q ≡ 1 [MOD p]
Catalan.CaseTwo.not_modEq_one_of_seven_weak_bound : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q < 180 → q ^ 6 ≡ 1 [MOD 49] → ¬q ≡ 1 [MOD 7]
Catalan.CaseTwo.not_modEq_one_of_five_weak_bound : ∀ (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q < 144 → q ^ 4 ≡ 1 [MOD 25] → 5 ^ (q - 1) ≡ 1 [MOD q ^ 2] → ¬q ≡ 1 [MOD 5]
Catalan.wieferich_lift_congruence : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → q ≡ 1 [MOD p] → q ^ (p - 1) ≡ 1 [MOD p ^ 2] → q ≡ 1 [MOD p ^ 2]

Catalan.θminus : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) → [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → ℕ → Catalan.R p K
Catalan.ΘS_coeff : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ) (a : (ZMod p)ˣ),
  (Catalan.ΘS p K k).coeff (Catalan.σ p K a)⁻¹ = ↑((↑a).val * k / p)
Catalan.θminus_coeff : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ) (a : (ZMod p)ˣ),
  (Catalan.θminus p K k).coeff (Catalan.σ p K a)⁻¹ =
    ↑((↑a).val * (k + 1) / p) - ↑((↑a).val * k / p) - (↑((↑(-a)).val * (k + 1) / p) - ↑((↑(-a)).val * k / p))
Catalan.θminus_eq_minusPart : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ),
  Catalan.θminus p K k = Catalan.A1e.minusPart p K (Catalan.ΘS p K (k + 1) - Catalan.ΘS p K k)
Catalan.θminus_weight : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ), Catalan.weight p K (Catalan.θminus p K k) = 0
Catalan.MinusGenerators.floor_step_zero_or_one : ∀ (p a k : ℕ),
  a < p → ↑(a * (k + 1) / p) - ↑(a * k / p) = 0 ∨ ↑(a * (k + 1) / p) - ↑(a * k / p) = 1
Catalan.MinusGenerators.complementary_floor_sum : ∀ (p a t : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → 0 < a → a < p → 0 < t → t < p → a * t / p + (p - a) * t / p = t - 1
Catalan.θminus_coeff_eq_neg_one_or_one : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ),
  1 ≤ k →
    k ≤ (p - 1) / 2 →
      ∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ),
        (Catalan.θminus p K k).coeff (Catalan.σ p K a)⁻¹ = -1 ∨ (Catalan.θminus p K k).coeff (Catalan.σ p K a)⁻¹ = 1
Catalan.θminus_size_eq : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ),
  1 ≤ k → k ≤ (p - 1) / 2 → Catalan.size p K (Catalan.θminus p K k) = ↑p - 1
Catalan.θminus_size_le : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ),
  1 ≤ k → k ≤ (p - 1) / 2 → Catalan.size p K (Catalan.θminus p K k) ≤ ↑p - 1
Catalan.ΘS_mul_single : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ) (b : (ZMod p)ˣ),
  Catalan.ΘS p K k * MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.σ p K b) 1 =
    Catalan.ΘS p K ((↑b).val * k) - ↑k • Catalan.ΘS p K (↑b).val
Catalan.pθ_mul_single : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (b : (ZMod p)ˣ),
  Catalan.pθ p K * MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.σ p K b) 1 = ↑(↑b).val • Catalan.pθ p K - ↑p • Catalan.ΘS p K (↑b).val
Catalan.stickSpan_mul_single : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  ∀ T ∈ Catalan.stickSpan p K, ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K), T * MonoidAlgebra.single τ 1 ∈ Catalan.stickSpan p K
Catalan.θminus_mem : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ), Catalan.θminus p K k ∈ Catalan.stickSpan p K
Catalan.θminus_small_three : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p = 3 → Catalan.θminus p K 1 = -(MonoidAlgebra.single 1 1 - MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.ι p K) 1)
Catalan.θminus_small_five : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p = 5 →
    Catalan.θminus p K 1 + Catalan.θminus p K 2 =
      -2 • (MonoidAlgebra.single 1 1 - MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.ι p K) 1)
Catalan.θminus_small_seven : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p = 7 →
    Catalan.θminus p K 2 + Catalan.θminus p K 3 =
      -2 • (MonoidAlgebra.single 1 1 - MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.ι p K) 1)
Catalan.minus_element_ne_zero : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 → MonoidAlgebra.single 1 1 - MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.ι p K) 1 ≠ 0
Catalan.minus_element_size : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 → Catalan.size p K (MonoidAlgebra.single 1 1 - MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.ι p K) 1) = 2
Catalan.q_smul_mem_mihIdeal : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (T : Catalan.R p K),
  ↑q • T ∈ Catalan.mihIdeal p K q x hp2
Catalan.θminus_mem_mihIdeal : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [hq : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → ∀ (k : ℕ), Catalan.θminus p K k ∈ Catalan.mihIdeal p K q x hp2
Catalan.small_minus_mem_mihAug : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [hq : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    p = 3 ∨ p = 5 ∨ p = 7 →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ),
        x ≠ 0 →
          y ≠ 0 →
            x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
              MonoidAlgebra.single 1 1 - MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.ι p K) 1 ∈ Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2
Catalan.small_conductor_aug_witness : ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [inst_2 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [hq : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    p = 3 ∨ p = 5 ∨ p = 7 →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → ∃ T ∈ Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2, T ≠ 0 ∧ Catalan.size p K T = 2
