Catalan.minusLinearMap : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Catalan.R p K →ₗ[ℤ] Catalan.R p K
Catalan.minusGeneratorSpan : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Submodule ℤ (Catalan.R p K)
Catalan.pθ_coeff : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), (Catalan.pθ p K).coeff (Catalan.σ p K a)⁻¹ = ↑(↑a).val
Catalan.ΘS_one : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K], Catalan.ΘS p K 1 = 0
Catalan.minus_theta_reflection : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (k : ℕ),
  1 ≤ k →
    k < p →
      (Catalan.minusLinearMap p K) (Catalan.ΘS p K k + Catalan.ΘS p K (p - k)) =
        (Catalan.minusLinearMap p K) (Catalan.pθ p K)
Catalan.theta_minus_spans_of_reflection : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  p ≠ 2 →
    (∀ (k : ℕ),
        1 ≤ k →
          k < p →
            (Catalan.minusLinearMap p K) (Catalan.ΘS p K k + Catalan.ΘS p K (p - k)) =
              (Catalan.minusLinearMap p K) (Catalan.pθ p K)) →
      Submodule.map (Catalan.minusLinearMap p K) (Catalan.stickSpan p K) = Catalan.minusGeneratorSpan p K
Catalan.theta_minus_spans : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  Submodule.map (Catalan.minusLinearMap p K) (Catalan.stickSpan p K) = Catalan.minusGeneratorSpan p K
Catalan.LFunction_zero_ne_zero_of_prime_odd : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ p),
  χ ≠ 1 → χ.Odd → DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ 0 ≠ 0
Catalan.LFunction_zero_eq_neg_weighted_sum_of_hurwitz_zero : (∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
    HurwitzZeta.hurwitzZeta (↑t) 0 = 1 / 2 - ↑t) →
  ∀ (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ p),
    χ ≠ 1 → DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ 0 = (-∑ a, ↑a.val * χ a) / ↑p
Catalan.odd_character_weighted_sum_ne_zero_of_hurwitz_zero : (∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
    HurwitzZeta.hurwitzZeta (↑t) 0 = 1 / 2 - ↑t) →
  ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ p), χ ≠ 1 → χ.Odd → ∑ a, ↑a.val * χ a ≠ 0
Catalan.hurwitzZeta_zero_eq_sinZeta_one_div_pi : ∀ (a : UnitAddCircle),
  a ≠ 0 → HurwitzZeta.hurwitzZeta a 0 = HurwitzZeta.sinZeta a 1 / ↑Real.pi
Catalan.hurwitzZeta_zero_eq_sine_value_of_mem_Ioo : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
  HurwitzZeta.hurwitzZeta (↑t) 0 = HurwitzZeta.sinZeta (↑t) 1 / ↑Real.pi
Catalan.cauchySeq_unit_circle_log_partial_sums : ∀ (z : ℂ),
  ‖z‖ = 1 → z ≠ 1 → CauchySeq fun N => ∑ n ∈ Finset.range N, z ^ (n + 1) / (↑n + 1)
Catalan.tendsto_unit_circle_log_partial_sums : ∀ (z : ℂ),
  ‖z‖ = 1 →
    z ≠ 1 → Filter.Tendsto (fun N => ∑ n ∈ Finset.range N, z ^ n / ↑n) Filter.atTop (nhds (-Complex.log (1 - z)))

Catalan.exp_two_pi_norm : ∀ (t : ℝ), ‖Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑t)‖ = 1
Catalan.exp_two_pi_ne_one : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑t) ≠ 1
Catalan.exp_two_pi_pow : ∀ (t : ℝ) (n : ℕ),
  Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑t) ^ n = Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑t * ↑n)
Catalan.unit_circle_dirichlet_tail_bound : ∀ (z : ℂ),
  ‖z‖ = 1 →
    z ≠ 1 → ∀ (s : ℝ), 1 ≤ s → ∀ (M N : ℕ), 1 ≤ M → M ≤ N → ‖∑ n ∈ Finset.Ico M N, z ^ n / ↑n ^ ↑s‖ ≤ 4 / (↑M * ‖1 - z‖)
Catalan.tendsto_expZeta_one_partial_sums_of_tail_bound : (∀ (z : ℂ),
    ‖z‖ = 1 →
      z ≠ 1 →
        ∀ (s : ℝ), 1 ≤ s → ∀ (M N : ℕ), 1 ≤ M → M ≤ N → ‖∑ n ∈ Finset.Ico M N, z ^ n / ↑n ^ ↑s‖ ≤ 4 / (↑M * ‖1 - z‖)) →
  ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
    Filter.Tendsto (fun N => ∑ n ∈ Finset.range N, Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑t * ↑n) / ↑n) Filter.atTop
      (nhds (HurwitzZeta.expZeta (↑t) 1))
Catalan.arg_one_sub_exp_two_pi : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
  (1 - Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑t)).arg = Real.pi * t - Real.pi / 2
Catalan.log_one_sub_exp_two_pi_difference : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
  -Complex.log (1 - Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑t)) +
      Complex.log (1 - Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑(1 - t))) =
    2 * Complex.I * (↑Real.pi * (1 / 2 - ↑t))
Catalan.tendsto_expZeta_one_partial_sums : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
  Filter.Tendsto (fun N => ∑ n ∈ Finset.range N, Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑t * ↑n) / ↑n) Filter.atTop
    (nhds (HurwitzZeta.expZeta (↑t) 1))
Catalan.expZeta_one_eq_neg_log : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
  HurwitzZeta.expZeta (↑t) 1 = -Complex.log (1 - Complex.exp (2 * ↑Real.pi * Complex.I * ↑t))
Catalan.sinZeta_one_eq_of_mem_Ioo : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, HurwitzZeta.sinZeta (↑t) 1 = ↑Real.pi * (1 / 2 - ↑t)
Catalan.hurwitzZeta_apply_zero_of_mem_Ioo : ∀ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, HurwitzZeta.hurwitzZeta (↑t) 0 = 1 / 2 - ↑t

  χ ≠ 1 → DirichletCharacter.LFunction χ 0 = (-∑ a, ↑a.val * χ a) / ↑p

  χ ≠ 1 → χ.Odd → ∑ a, ↑a.val * χ a ≠ 0

Catalan.MinusIndependence.ComplexRing : ℕ → (K : Type u_1) → [inst : Field K] → [NumberField K] → Type u_1
Catalan.MinusIndependence.coeffCast : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → Catalan.R p K →+* Catalan.MinusIndependence.ComplexRing p K
Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterOnG : (p : ℕ) →
  [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → DirichletCharacter ℂ p → Catalan.G p K →* ℂ
Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterEval : (p : ℕ) →
  [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
          [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → DirichletCharacter ℂ p → Catalan.MinusIndependence.ComplexRing p K →ₐ[ℂ] ℂ
Catalan.MinusIndependence.complexPTheta : (p : ℕ) →
  [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Catalan.MinusIndependence.ComplexRing p K
Catalan.MinusIndependence.complexMinus : (p : ℕ) →
  [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Catalan.MinusIndependence.ComplexRing p K
Catalan.MinusIndependence.minusRange : (p : ℕ) →
  [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
          [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Submodule ℂ (Catalan.MinusIndependence.ComplexRing p K)
Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfUnit : (p : ℕ) → [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] → Fin ((p - 1) / 2) → (ZMod p)ˣ
Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfVector : (p : ℕ) →
  [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
          [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Fin ((p - 1) / 2) → Catalan.MinusIndependence.ComplexRing p K
Catalan.MinusIndependence.thetaVector : (p : ℕ) →
  [hp : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
          [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Fin ((p - 1) / 2) → Catalan.MinusIndependence.ComplexRing p K
Catalan.MinusIndependence.coeffCast_coeff : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (T : Catalan.R p K) (g : Catalan.G p K), ((Catalan.MinusIndependence.coeffCast p K) T).coeff g = ↑(T.coeff g)
Catalan.MinusIndependence.coeffCast_single : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (g : Catalan.G p K) (a : ℤ),
  (Catalan.MinusIndependence.coeffCast p K) (MonoidAlgebra.single g a) = MonoidAlgebra.single g ↑a
Catalan.MinusIndependence.coeffCast_injective : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K],
  Function.Injective ⇑(Catalan.MinusIndependence.coeffCast p K)
Catalan.unit_character_recovery : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (c : (ZMod p)ˣ → ℂ) (b : (ZMod p)ˣ),
  ∑ χ, χ (↑b)⁻¹ * ∑ a, c a * χ ↑a = ↑p.totient * c b
Catalan.unit_character_transform_injective : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)],
  Function.Injective fun c χ => ∑ a, c a * χ ↑a
Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterOnG_sigma_inv : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ p)
  (a : (ZMod p)ˣ), (Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterOnG p K χ) (Catalan.σ p K a)⁻¹ = χ ↑a
Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterEval_coeff : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ p)
  (T : Catalan.MinusIndependence.ComplexRing p K),
  (Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterEval p K χ) T = ∑ a, T.coeff (Catalan.σ p K a)⁻¹ * χ ↑a
Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterEval_PTheta : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ p),
  (Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterEval p K χ) (Catalan.MinusIndependence.complexPTheta p K) = ∑ a, ↑a.val * χ a
Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterEval_minus : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ p),
  (Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterEval p K χ) (Catalan.MinusIndependence.complexMinus p K) = 1 - χ (-1)
Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterEval_joint_injective : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  Function.Injective fun T χ => (Catalan.MinusIndependence.characterEval p K χ) T
Catalan.MinusIndependence.complexPTheta_mul_eq_zero : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  ∀ T ∈ Catalan.MinusIndependence.minusRange p K, Catalan.MinusIndependence.complexPTheta p K * T = 0 → T = 0
Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfVector_coeff : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (i j : Fin ((p - 1) / 2)),
  (Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfVector p K i).coeff (Catalan.σ p K (Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfUnit p j))⁻¹ =
    if i = j then 1 else 0
Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfVector_linearIndependent : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  LinearIndependent ℂ (Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfVector p K)
Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfVector_mem_minusRange : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (i : Fin ((p - 1) / 2)),
  Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfVector p K i ∈ Catalan.MinusIndependence.minusRange p K
Catalan.MinusIndependence.coeffCast_mem_theta_span : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  ∀ T ∈ Catalan.minusGeneratorSpan p K,
    (Catalan.MinusIndependence.coeffCast p K) T ∈
      Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (Catalan.MinusIndependence.thetaVector p K))
Catalan.MinusIndependence.complexPTheta_mul_halfVector_mem_span : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (i : Fin ((p - 1) / 2)),
  Catalan.MinusIndependence.complexPTheta p K * Catalan.MinusIndependence.halfVector p K i ∈
    Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (Catalan.MinusIndependence.thetaVector p K))
Catalan.MinusIndependence.thetaVector_linearIndependent : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  LinearIndependent ℂ (Catalan.MinusIndependence.thetaVector p K)
Catalan.θminus_linIndep : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  Function.Injective fun c => ∑ k, c k • Catalan.θminus p K (↑k + 1)

Catalan.size_zsmul : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (z : ℤ) (T : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.size p K (z • T) = |z| * Catalan.size p K T
Catalan.weight_zsmul : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (z : ℤ) (T : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.weight p K (z • T) = z * Catalan.weight p K T
Catalan.size_sum_le : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] {ι : Type u_2} (s : Finset ι)
  (f : ι → Catalan.R p K), Catalan.size p K (∑ i ∈ s, f i) ≤ ∑ i ∈ s, Catalan.size p K (f i)
Catalan.weight_sum : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] {ι : Type u_2} (s : Finset ι)
  (f : ι → Catalan.R p K), Catalan.weight p K (∑ i ∈ s, f i) = ∑ i ∈ s, Catalan.weight p K (f i)
Catalan.thetaCombination : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → (Fin ((p - 1) / 2) → ℤ) → Catalan.R p K
Catalan.thetaCombination_injective : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K], Function.Injective (Catalan.thetaCombination p K)
Catalan.thetaCombination_weight : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (c : Fin ((p - 1) / 2) → ℤ),
  Catalan.weight p K (Catalan.thetaCombination p K c) = 0
Catalan.thetaCombination_size : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (c : Fin ((p - 1) / 2) → ℤ),
  Catalan.size p K (Catalan.thetaCombination p K c) ≤ (↑p - 1) * ∑ i, |c i|
Catalan.thetaCombination_mem_mihIdeal : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (x y : ℤ),
      x ≠ 0 →
        y ≠ 0 →
          x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
            ∀ (c : Fin ((p - 1) / 2) → ℤ), Catalan.thetaCombination p K c ∈ Catalan.mihIdeal p K q x hp2
Catalan.thetaCombination_mem_augBall : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (x y : ℤ),
      x ≠ 0 →
        y ≠ 0 →
          x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
            ∀ (r : ℕ) (c : Fin ((p - 1) / 2) → ℤ),
              ∑ i, |c i| ≤ ↑r → Catalan.thetaCombination p K c ∈ Catalan.augBall p K q x hp2 ((↑p - 1) * ↑r)
Catalan.S_le_card_augBall : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (x y : ℤ),
      x ≠ 0 →
        y ≠ 0 →
          x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → ∀ (r : ℕ), Catalan.S ((p - 1) / 2) r ≤ (Catalan.augBall p K q x hp2 ((↑p - 1) * ↑r)).ncard
Catalan.S_le_card_augBall_quotient : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (x y : ℤ),
      x ≠ 0 →
        y ≠ 0 →
          x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
            Catalan.S ((p - 1) / 2) (q / (p - 1) ^ 2) ≤ (Catalan.augBall p K q x hp2 (↑q / (↑p - 1))).ncard
Catalan.q_lt_four_sq_of_aug_card_upper : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    11 ≤ p →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ),
        x ≠ 0 →
          y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → (Catalan.augBall p K q x hp2 (↑q / (↑p - 1))).ncard ≤ q → q < 4 * (p - 1) ^ 2
Catalan.q_lt_180_of_aug_card_upper : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    p = 7 →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → (Catalan.augBall p K q x hp2 (↑q / (↑p - 1))).ncard ≤ q → q < 180
Catalan.q_lt_144_of_aug_card_upper : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q ≠ 2 →
    p = 5 →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → (Catalan.augBall p K q x hp2 (↑q / (↑p - 1))).ncard ≤ q → q < 144
Catalan.q_lt_four_sq_of_lattice_upper : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → 11 ≤ p → Catalan.S ((p - 1) / 2) (q / (p - 1) ^ 2) ≤ q → q < 4 * (p - 1) ^ 2
Catalan.q_lt_180_of_lattice_upper : ∀ (q : ℕ), Catalan.S 3 (q / 36) ≤ q → q < 180
Catalan.q_lt_144_of_lattice_upper : ∀ (q : ℕ), Catalan.S 2 (q / 16) ≤ q → q < 144
Catalan.quotient_radius_le : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 1 < p → (↑p - 1) * ↑(q / (p - 1) ^ 2) ≤ ↑q / (↑p - 1)
Catalan.hyyro_implies_h8_one : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q →
      5 ≤ p →
        p < q →
          ∀ (x y : ℤ),
            x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → max (Catalan.mihThreshold p 1) (4 / Real.pi * ↑q / (↑p - 1) + 1) ≤ |↑x|
Catalan.hyyro_implies_h9 : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → p < q → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → 8 * (4 / 5 * ↑q) ^ q ≤ |↑x|
Catalan.cassels_x_mod_one : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p → Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → x ≡ 1 [ZMOD ↑p]

Catalan.augBall_eq_singleton_of_lt_two : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (r : ℝ), 0 ≤ r → r < 2 → Catalan.augBall p K q x hp2 r = {0}
Catalan.card_aug_ball_le_of_q_eq_two : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  q = 2 → ∀ (ε : ℝ), 0 < ε → ε ≤ 1 → (Catalan.augBall p K q x hp2 ((2 - ε) * ↑q / (↑p - 1))).ncard ≤ q
'Catalan.augBall_eq_singleton_of_lt_two' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
'Catalan.card_aug_ball_le_of_q_eq_two' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Catalan.logHeight : {L : Type u_1} → [inst : Field L] → [NumberField L] → L → ℝ
@Catalan.logHeight_eq_normalized : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (a : L),
  Catalan.logHeight a = (↑(Module.finrank ℚ L))⁻¹ * Height.logHeight₁ a
@Catalan.height_pow : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (a : L) (n : ℕ),
  Catalan.logHeight (a ^ n) = ↑n * Catalan.logHeight a
@Catalan.height_inv : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (a : L),
  Catalan.logHeight a⁻¹ = Catalan.logHeight a
@Catalan.height_sub_le : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (a b : L),
  Catalan.logHeight (a - b) ≤ Catalan.logHeight a + Catalan.logHeight b + Real.log 2
@Catalan.height_div_le : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (a b : L),
  Catalan.logHeight (a / b) ≤ Catalan.logHeight a + Catalan.logHeight b
@Catalan.height_one : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L], Catalan.logHeight 1 = 0
@Catalan.height_intCast : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (x : ℤ),
  x ≠ 0 → Catalan.logHeight ↑x = Real.log |↑x|
@Catalan.height_root_unity : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (a : L) (n : ℕ),
  0 < n → a ^ n = 1 → Catalan.logHeight a = 0
@Catalan.height_projective_integral_le : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (a b : L),
  a ≠ 0 →
    b ≠ 0 →
      IsIntegral ℤ a →
        IsIntegral ℤ b →
          ∀ (C : ℝ),
            1 ≤ C → (∀ (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace L), max (w a) (w b) ≤ C) → Catalan.logHeight (a / b) ≤ Real.log C
@Catalan.height_liouville_complex : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (φ : L →+* ℂ),
  (NumberField.InfinitePlace.mk φ).IsComplex →
    ∀ (a : L), a ≠ 0 → Real.exp (-(↑(Module.finrank ℚ L) * Catalan.logHeight a)) ≤ ‖φ a‖ ^ 2
@Catalan.height_lt_of_local_bounds : ∀ {L : Type u_1} [inst : Field L] [inst_1 : NumberField L] (a : L) (C D : ℝ),
  1 < C →
    (∀ (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace L), w a < C) →
      ∑ᶠ (v : NumberField.FinitePlace L), Real.log (max (v a) 1) ≤ D →
        Catalan.logHeight a < Real.log C + D / ↑(Module.finrank ℚ L)
Catalan.upow_xmζ_eq_nat_prod : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (Θ : Catalan.R p K),
  (∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K), 0 ≤ Θ.coeff τ) →
    ↑(Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.xmζ p K x hp2) Θ) = ∏ τ, (↑x - τ (Catalan.ζ p K)) ^ (Θ.coeff τ).toNat
Catalan.integral_upow_xmζ_of_nonneg : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (Θ : Catalan.R p K),
  (∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K), 0 ≤ Θ.coeff τ) → IsIntegral ℤ ↑(Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.xmζ p K x hp2) Θ)
Catalan.sum_coeff_toNat_eq_size : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (Θ : Catalan.R p K), (∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K), 0 ≤ Θ.coeff τ) → ∑ τ, (Θ.coeff τ).toNat = (Catalan.size p K Θ).toNat
Catalan.infinitePlace_upow_xmζ_le : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (Θ : Catalan.R p K),
  (∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K), 0 ≤ Θ.coeff τ) →
    ∀ (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K),
      w ↑(Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.xmζ p K x hp2) Θ) ≤ (|↑x| + 1) ^ (Catalan.size p K Θ).toNat
Catalan.prop44_height : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (Θ : Catalan.R p K),
  Catalan.logHeight ↑(Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.xmζ p K x hp2) Θ) ≤
    (↑(Catalan.size p K Θ) + |↑(Catalan.weight p K Θ)|) / 2 * Real.log (|↑x| + 1)
Catalan.prop47_height : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
  (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihIdeal p K q x hp2)),
  Catalan.logHeight ↑(Catalan.alpha p K q x hp2 Θ) ≤
    (↑(Catalan.size p K ↑Θ) + |↑(Catalan.weight p K ↑Θ)|) / (2 * ↑q) * Real.log (|↑x| + 1)
Catalan.phase_kernel : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K]
  [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (φ : K →+* ℂ) (ε : ℝ),
        0 < ε →
          ε ≤ 1 →
            |↑x| ≥ max (Catalan.mihThreshold p ε) (4 / Real.pi * ↑q / (↑p - 1) + 1) →
              ∀ (hx : 1 < |↑x|) (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
                ↑(Catalan.size p K ↑Θ) ≤ 2 * Catalan.mihRadius p q ε → Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx Θ = 1 → ↑Θ = 0
Catalan.phase_injective_ball : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ)
  (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (φ : K →+* ℂ) (ε : ℝ),
        0 < ε →
          ε ≤ 1 →
            |↑x| ≥ max (Catalan.mihThreshold p ε) (4 / Real.pi * ↑q / (↑p - 1) + 1) →
              ∀ (hx : 1 < |↑x|), Function.Injective fun Θ => Catalan.xi p K q x φ hp2 hx ⟨↑Θ, ⋯⟩
Catalan.card_aug_ball_le : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    ∀ (ε : ℝ),
      0 < ε →
        ε ≤ 1 →
          |↑x| ≥ max ((36 * 2 ^ (p - 1) / (↑p - 1) ^ 2) ^ (1 / ε)) (4 / Real.pi * ↑q / (↑p - 1) + 1) →
            {Θ |
                  Θ ∈ Catalan.mihIdeal p K q x hp2 ∧
                    Catalan.weight p K Θ = 0 ∧ ↑(Catalan.size p K Θ) ≤ (2 - ε) * ↑q / (↑p - 1)}.ncard ≤
              q
Catalan.prop42_reconstruct_height : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  x ≠ 0 →
    ∀ (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
      Catalan.size p K ↑Θ ≤ 2 →
        ↑Θ ≠ 0 → Real.log |↑x| ≤ ↑q * Catalan.logHeight ↑(Catalan.augAlpha p K q x hp2 Θ) + 3 * Real.log 2
Catalan.prop42_arch_bound : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (ε : ℝ),
        0 < ε →
          ε ≤ 1 →
            ↑p ≤ (2 - ε) * ↑q + 1 →
              |↑x| ≥ max (Catalan.mihThreshold p ε) (4 / Real.pi * ↑q / (↑p - 1) + 1) →
                ∀ (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
                  Catalan.size p K ↑Θ ≤ 2 →
                    ↑Θ ≠ 0 → ∀ (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K), 5 / 2 / ↑q < w (↑(Catalan.augAlpha p K q x hp2 Θ) - 1)
Catalan.prop42_height_lt : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (ε : ℝ),
        0 < ε →
          ε ≤ 1 →
            ↑p ≤ (2 - ε) * ↑q + 1 →
              |↑x| ≥ max (Catalan.mihThreshold p ε) (4 / Real.pi * ↑q / (↑p - 1) + 1) →
                ∀ (Θ : ↥(Catalan.mihAug p K q x hp2)),
                  Catalan.size p K ↑Θ ≤ 2 →
                    ↑Θ ≠ 0 →
                      Catalan.logHeight ↑(Catalan.augAlpha p K q x hp2 Θ) <
                        Real.log (0.8 * ↑q) + Real.log ↑(Catalan.pPrime p x) / (↑p - 1)
Catalan.aug_two_eq_zero_of_h9 : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (ε : ℝ),
        0 < ε →
          ε ≤ 1 →
            ↑p ≤ (2 - ε) * ↑q + 1 →
              |↑x| ≥ max (Catalan.mihThreshold p ε) (8 * (0.8 * ↑q * ↑(Catalan.pPrime p x) ^ (1 / (↑p - 1))) ^ q) →
                ∀ Θ ∈ Catalan.mihIdeal p K q x hp2, Catalan.weight p K Θ = 0 → Catalan.size p K Θ ≤ 2 → Θ = 0
Catalan.aug_two_eq_zero : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (x : ℤ) (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p < q →
    x ≡ 1 [ZMOD ↑p] →
      |↑x| ≥ 8 * (0.8 * ↑q) ^ q →
        ∀ Θ ∈ Catalan.mihIdeal p K q x hp2, Catalan.weight p K Θ = 0 → Catalan.size p K Θ ≤ 2 → Θ = 0

Catalan.small_odd_prime_no_solution : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → p < q → p = 3 ∨ p = 5 ∨ p = 7 → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p ≠ y ^ q + 1

  Nat.Prime p →
    Nat.Prime q → p ≠ 2 → q ≠ 2 → 11 ≤ p → ∀ (x y : ℤ), x ≠ 0 → y ≠ 0 → x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 → q < 4 * (p - 1) ^ 2

Catalan.A3.isTotallyReal_F : ∀ (p : ℕ), 0 < p → NumberField.IsTotallyReal (Catalan.A3.F p)
Catalan.A3.isAbelianGalois_F : ∀ (p : ℕ), 0 < p → IsAbelianGalois ℚ (Catalan.A3.F p)
Catalan.A3.finrank_F : ∀ (p : ℕ), Nat.Prime p → p ≠ 2 → Module.finrank ℚ (Catalan.A3.F p) = (p - 1) / 2
Catalan.A3.gal_F_cyclic : ∀ (p : ℕ), Nat.Prime p → IsCyclic Gal(Catalan.A3.F p/ℚ)
Catalan.A3.card_gal_F : ∀ (p : ℕ), Nat.Prime p → p ≠ 2 → Nat.card Gal(Catalan.A3.F p/ℚ) = (p - 1) / 2
Catalan.Circular.exists_ratio_unit : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (a b : (ZMod p)ˣ),
  ∃ u, ↑↑u = (1 - Catalan.ζ p K ^ (↑a).val) / (1 - Catalan.ζ p K ^ (↑b).val)
Catalan.UnitQuotient.qPowers : (B : Type u_1) → [inst : CommGroup B] → ℕ → Subgroup B
Catalan.UnitQuotient.ModSquare : (A : Type u_1) → [CommRing A] → ℕ → Type u_1
Catalan.UnitQuotient.modSquareUnit : (A : Type u_1) →
  [inst : CommRing A] → (q : ℕ) → Aˣ →* (Catalan.UnitQuotient.ModSquare A q)ˣ
Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits : (A : Type u_1) → [inst : CommRing A] → ℕ → Subgroup Aˣ
Catalan.UnitQuotient.mem_primaryUnits_iff : ∀ (A : Type u_1) [inst : CommRing A] (q : ℕ),
  0 < q → ∀ (u : Aˣ), u ∈ Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits A q ↔ ∃ v, ↑q ^ 2 ∣ ↑u - v ^ q
Catalan.UnitQuotient.qPowers_le_primaryUnits : ∀ (A : Type u_1) [inst : CommRing A] (q : ℕ),
  Catalan.UnitQuotient.qPowers Aˣ q ≤ Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits A q
Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits_map : ∀ (A : Type u_1) (B : Type u_2) [inst : CommRing A] [inst_1 : CommRing B]
  (q : ℕ),
  0 < q →
    ∀ (f : A →+* B),
      ∀ u ∈ Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits A q, (Units.map ↑f) u ∈ Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits B q
Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits_map_equiv_iff : ∀ (A : Type u_1) [inst : CommRing A] (q : ℕ),
  0 < q →
    ∀ (e : A ≃+* A) (u : Aˣ),
      (Units.map e.toMonoidHom) u ∈ Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits A q ↔ u ∈ Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits A q
Catalan.Circular.unitAction : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] →
      [inst_1 : NumberField K] → Catalan.G p K → (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ →* (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ
Catalan.Circular.unitAction_coe : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (τ : Catalan.G p K)
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ), ↑↑((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u) = τ ↑↑u
Catalan.Circular.circularUnits : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → Subgroup (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ
Catalan.Circular.primaryCircularUnits : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] → [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → ℕ → Subgroup (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ
Catalan.Circular.exists_zeta_sum_circular : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q < p → ∃ u ∈ Catalan.Circular.circularUnits p K, ↑u = 1 + ⋯.toInteger ^ q
Catalan.Circular.primaryCircularUnits_ne : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q → q < p → Catalan.Circular.primaryCircularUnits p K q ≠ Catalan.Circular.circularUnits p K
Catalan.Circular.circularUnits_stable : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (τ : Catalan.G p K),
  ∀ u ∈ Catalan.Circular.circularUnits p K, (Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u ∈ Catalan.Circular.circularUnits p K
Catalan.Circular.primaryCircularUnits_stable : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  0 < q →
    ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K),
      ∀ u ∈ Catalan.Circular.primaryCircularUnits p K q,
        (Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u ∈ Catalan.Circular.primaryCircularUnits p K q

Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient : (B : Type u_1) → [CommGroup B] → ℕ → Type u_1
@Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass : {B : Type u_1} →
  [inst : CommGroup B] → (q : ℕ) → B → Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient B q
@Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerQuotient_exponent : ∀ {B : Type u_1} [inst : CommGroup B] (q : ℕ)
  (v : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient B q), q • v = 0
Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerQuotientModule : (B : Type u_1) →
  [inst : CommGroup B] → (q : ℕ) → Module (ZMod q) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient B q)
@Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerMap : {B : Type u_1} →
  {D : Type u_2} →
    [inst : CommGroup B] →
      [inst_1 : CommGroup D] →
        (q : ℕ) → (B →* D) → Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient B q →ₗ[ZMod q] Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient D q
@Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerMap_apply : ∀ {B : Type u_1} {D : Type u_2} [inst : CommGroup B] [inst_1 : CommGroup D]
  (q : ℕ) (f : B →* D) (b : B),
  (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerMap q f) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q b) = Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q (f b)
Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerImage : (B : Type u_1) →
  [inst : CommGroup B] → (q : ℕ) → Subgroup B → Submodule (ZMod q) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient B q)
Catalan.UnitQuotient.mem_powerImage_iff : ∀ (B : Type u_1) [inst : CommGroup B] (q : ℕ) (C : Subgroup B)
  (v : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient B q),
  v ∈ Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerImage B q C ↔ ∃ c ∈ C, Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q c = v
Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerImage_inf_eq_iff : ∀ (B : Type u_1) [inst : CommGroup B] (q : ℕ) (C H : Subgroup B),
  Catalan.UnitQuotient.qPowers B q ≤ H →
    (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerImage B q (C ⊓ H) = Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerImage B q C ↔ C ≤ H)
Catalan.UnitQuotient.unitTorsionMap : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [NumberField K] →
      (q : ℕ) →
        Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ q →ₗ[ZMod q]
          Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient ((NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ ⧸ NumberField.Units.torsion K) q
Catalan.UnitQuotient.torsion_le_qPowers : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ q → q ≠ 2 → NumberField.Units.torsion K ≤ Catalan.UnitQuotient.qPowers (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ q
Catalan.UnitQuotient.unitTorsionMap_bijective : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ q → q ≠ 2 → Function.Bijective ⇑(Catalan.UnitQuotient.unitTorsionMap K q)
Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] →
      [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
        (q : ℕ) →
          Representation (ZMod q) (Catalan.G p K) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ q)
Catalan.UnitModule.UnitPowerModule : ℕ → (K : Type u_1) → [inst : Field K] → [NumberField K] → ℕ → Type u_1
Catalan.UnitModule.unitClass : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] →
      [inst_1 : NumberField K] → (q : ℕ) → (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ → Catalan.UnitModule.UnitPowerModule p K q
Catalan.UnitModule.unitClass_surjective : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (q : ℕ),
  Function.Surjective (Catalan.UnitModule.unitClass p K q)
Catalan.UnitModule.single_smul_unitClass : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (q : ℕ)
  (τ : Catalan.G p K) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
  MonoidAlgebra.single τ 1 • Catalan.UnitModule.unitClass p K q u =
    Catalan.UnitModule.unitClass p K q ((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u)
Catalan.UnitModule.stableUnitImage : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] →
      [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
        (q : ℕ) →
          (S : Subgroup (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ) →
            (∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K), ∀ u ∈ S, (Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u ∈ S) →
              Submodule (MonoidAlgebra (ZMod q) (Catalan.G p K)) (Catalan.UnitModule.UnitPowerModule p K q)
Catalan.UnitModule.mem_stableUnitImage_iff : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (q : ℕ)
  (S : Subgroup (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ)
  (hS : ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K), ∀ u ∈ S, (Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u ∈ S)
  (v : Catalan.UnitModule.UnitPowerModule p K q),
  v ∈ Catalan.UnitModule.stableUnitImage p K q S hS ↔ ∃ u ∈ S, Catalan.UnitModule.unitClass p K q u = v
Catalan.UnitModule.circularImage : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] →
      [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
        (q : ℕ) →
          [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
            [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] →
              Submodule (MonoidAlgebra (ZMod q) (Catalan.G p K)) (Catalan.UnitModule.UnitPowerModule p K q)
Catalan.UnitModule.primaryCircularImage : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] →
      [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
        (q : ℕ) →
          [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
            [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] →
              0 < q → Submodule (MonoidAlgebra (ZMod q) (Catalan.G p K)) (Catalan.UnitModule.UnitPowerModule p K q)
Catalan.UnitModule.primaryCircularImage_le : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (q : ℕ)
  [inst_2 : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (hq : 0 < q),
  Catalan.UnitModule.primaryCircularImage p K q hq ≤ Catalan.UnitModule.circularImage p K q
Catalan.UnitModule.primaryCircularImage_ne : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (q : ℕ)
  [inst_2 : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (hq : Nat.Prime q),
  q < p → Catalan.UnitModule.primaryCircularImage p K q ⋯ ≠ Catalan.UnitModule.circularImage p K q
Catalan.UnitModule.primaryCircularImage_lt : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (q : ℕ)
  [inst_2 : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (hq : Nat.Prime q),
  q < p → Catalan.UnitModule.primaryCircularImage p K q ⋯ < Catalan.UnitModule.circularImage p K q
Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] → (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ → NumberField.InfinitePlace K → ℝ
Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] → [NumberField K] → Submodule ℝ (NumberField.InfinitePlace K → ℝ)
Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog_one : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [NumberField K], Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K 1 = 0
Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog_mul : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [NumberField K] (u v : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
  Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K (u * v) = Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K u + Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K v
Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog_sum : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ), ∑ w, Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K u w = 0
Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog_eq_zero_iff : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [NumberField K]
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ), Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K u = 0 ↔ u ∈ NumberField.Units.torsion K
Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog_span : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K],
  Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K)) = Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K
Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog_unitAction : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (p : ℕ)
  (τ : Catalan.G p K) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ) (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K),
  Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K ((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u) w =
    Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K u (w.comap τ.toRingEquiv.toRingHom)

Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerQuotientModN : (B : Type u_1) →
  [inst : CommGroup B] → (q : ℕ) → Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient B q ≃ₗ[ZMod q] ModN (Additive B) q

  (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerQuotientModN B q) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q b) = (ModN.mkQ q) (Additive.ofMul b)

  [inst : Field K] →
    [NumberField K] →
      (NumberField.RingOfIntegers ↥(NumberField.maximalRealSubfield K))ˣ →* (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ

  [inst : Field K] →
    [NumberField K] →

Catalan.UnitQuotient.realUnitPowerMap_bijective : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K],
  2 < p →
    ∀ (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)], p ≠ q → q ≠ 2 → Function.Bijective ⇑(Catalan.UnitQuotient.realUnitPowerMap K q)
Catalan.UnitReduction.modNMap : (M : Type u_1) →
  [inst : AddCommGroup M] → (q : ℕ) → (M →ₗ[ℤ] M) → ModN M q →ₗ[ZMod q] ModN M q
Catalan.UnitReduction.modNMap_apply : ∀ (M : Type u_1) [inst : AddCommGroup M] (q : ℕ) (f : M →ₗ[ℤ] M) (x : M),
  (Catalan.UnitReduction.modNMap M q f) ((ModN.mkQ q) x) = (ModN.mkQ q) (f x)
Catalan.UnitReduction.modNMap_toMatrix : ∀ (M : Type u_1) [inst : AddCommGroup M] (q : ℕ) [inst_1 : NeZero q]
  {ι : Type u_2} [inst_2 : Fintype ι] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ι] (b : Module.Basis ι ℤ M) (f : M →ₗ[ℤ] M),
  (LinearMap.toMatrix (ModN.basis b) (ModN.basis b)) (Catalan.UnitReduction.modNMap M q f) =
    ((LinearMap.toMatrix b b) f).map ⇑(Int.castRingHom (ZMod q))
Catalan.UnitReduction.modNModuleFree : ∀ (M : Type u_1) [inst : AddCommGroup M] (q : ℕ) [NeZero q] [Module.Free ℤ M],
  Module.Free (ZMod q) (ModN M q)
Catalan.UnitReduction.modNMap_charpoly : ∀ (M : Type u_1) [inst : AddCommGroup M] (q : ℕ) [inst_1 : NeZero q]
  [inst_2 : Module.Free ℤ M] [inst_3 : Module.Finite ℤ M] (f : M →ₗ[ℤ] M),
  (Catalan.UnitReduction.modNMap M q f).charpoly = Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom (ZMod q)) f.charpoly
@Catalan.UnitLog.charpoly_eq_map_of_lattice_intertwining : ∀ {E : Type u_1} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E] [inst_2 : FiniteDimensional ℝ E] (L : Submodule ℤ E) [inst_3 : DiscreteTopology ↥L]
  [IsZLattice ℝ L] [inst_5 : Module.Free ℤ ↥L] [inst_6 : Module.Finite ℤ ↥L] (f : ↥L →ₗ[ℤ] ↥L) (g : E →ₗ[ℝ] E),
  (∀ (x : ↥L), g ↑x = ↑(f x)) → g.charpoly = Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) f.charpoly
Catalan.UnitLog.logSpaceLift : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] → NumberField.Units.dirichletUnitTheorem.logSpace K →ₗ[ℝ] NumberField.InfinitePlace K → ℝ
Catalan.UnitLog.logSpaceLift_mem : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (f : NumberField.Units.dirichletUnitTheorem.logSpace K),
  (Catalan.UnitLog.logSpaceLift K) f ∈ Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K
Catalan.UnitLog.logSpaceEquiv : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] → NumberField.Units.dirichletUnitTheorem.logSpace K ≃ₗ[ℝ] ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K)
Catalan.UnitLog.logSpaceEquiv_logEmbedding : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
  ↑((Catalan.UnitLog.logSpaceEquiv K) ((NumberField.Units.logEmbedding K) (Additive.ofMul u))) =
    Catalan.UnitLog.fullLog K u
Catalan.UnitLog.placeEquiv : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] → (p : ℕ) → Catalan.G p K → NumberField.InfinitePlace K ≃ NumberField.InfinitePlace K
Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSumAction : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] → (p : ℕ) → Catalan.G p K → ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K) →ₗ[ℝ] ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K)
Catalan.UnitLog.logRealAction : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
      (p : ℕ) →
        Catalan.G p K →
          NumberField.Units.dirichletUnitTheorem.logSpace K →ₗ[ℝ] NumberField.Units.dirichletUnitTheorem.logSpace K
Catalan.UnitLog.logRealAction_logEmbedding : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (p : ℕ)
  (τ : Catalan.G p K) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
  (Catalan.UnitLog.logRealAction K p τ) ((NumberField.Units.logEmbedding K) (Additive.ofMul u)) =
    (NumberField.Units.logEmbedding K) (Additive.ofMul ((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u))
Catalan.UnitLog.gal_place_bijective : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] [IsGalois ℚ K] (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K),
  Function.Bijective fun σ => w.comap σ.toRingEquiv.toRingHom
Catalan.UnitLog.galPlaceEquiv : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
      [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] →
        [IsGalois ℚ K] → NumberField.InfinitePlace K → Gal(K/ℚ) ≃ NumberField.InfinitePlace K
Catalan.UnitLog.galPlaceEquiv_mul : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] [inst_3 : IsGalois ℚ K] (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K) (σ τ : Gal(K/ℚ)),
  (Catalan.UnitLog.galPlaceEquiv K w) (σ * τ) = ((Catalan.UnitLog.galPlaceEquiv K w) σ).comap τ.toRingEquiv.toRingHom
Catalan.UnitLog.groupZeroSum : (K : Type u_1) → [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → Submodule ℝ (Gal(K/ℚ) → ℝ)
Catalan.UnitLog.regularZeroSumAction : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] → Gal(K/ℚ) → ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.groupZeroSum K) →ₗ[ℝ] ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.groupZeroSum K)
Catalan.UnitLog.regularZeroSumAction_apply : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (τ : Gal(K/ℚ))
  (f : ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.groupZeroSum K)) (σ : Gal(K/ℚ)), ↑((Catalan.UnitLog.regularZeroSumAction K τ) f) σ = ↑f (σ * τ)
Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSumRegularEquiv : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
      [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] →
        [IsGalois ℚ K] →
          NumberField.InfinitePlace K → ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K) ≃ₗ[ℝ] ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.groupZeroSum K)
Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSumRegularEquiv_apply : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] [inst_3 : IsGalois ℚ K] (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K)
  (f : ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K)) (σ : Gal(K/ℚ)),
  ↑((Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSumRegularEquiv K w) f) σ = ↑f ((Catalan.UnitLog.galPlaceEquiv K w) σ)
Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSumRegularEquiv_intertwining : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] [inst_3 : IsGalois ℚ K] (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K) (p : ℕ)
  (τ : Catalan.G p K) (f : ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K)),
  (Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSumRegularEquiv K w) ((Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSumAction K p τ) f) =
    (Catalan.UnitLog.regularZeroSumAction K τ) ((Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSumRegularEquiv K w) f)
Catalan.UnitModule.UnitLattice : (K : Type u_1) → [inst : Field K] → [NumberField K] → Type u_1

  [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → Module ℤ (Catalan.UnitModule.UnitLattice K)
Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitClass : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ → Catalan.UnitModule.UnitLattice K
Catalan.UnitModule.torsionAction : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] →
      [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
        Catalan.G p K →
          (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ ⧸ NumberField.Units.torsion K →*
            (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ ⧸ NumberField.Units.torsion K
Catalan.UnitModule.torsionAction_apply : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (τ : Catalan.G p K) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
  (Catalan.UnitModule.torsionAction p K τ) ↑u = ↑((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u)
Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitRepresentation : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] → [inst_1 : NumberField K] → Representation ℤ (Catalan.G p K) (Catalan.UnitModule.UnitLattice K)
Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitRepresentation_apply : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (τ : Catalan.G p K) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
  ((Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitRepresentation p K) τ) (Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitClass K u) =
    Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitClass K ((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ) u)
Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitClass_surjective : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K],
  Function.Surjective (Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitClass K)
Catalan.UnitModule.unitTorsionMap_equivariant : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (q : ℕ) (τ : Catalan.G p K) (v : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ q),
  (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerMap q (Catalan.UnitModule.torsionAction p K τ))
      ((Catalan.UnitQuotient.unitTorsionMap K q) v) =
    (Catalan.UnitQuotient.unitTorsionMap K q) ((Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerMap q (Catalan.Circular.unitAction p K τ)) v)

  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] →
      [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
        Catalan.G p K → ↥(NumberField.Units.unitLattice K) →ₗ[ℤ] ↥(NumberField.Units.unitLattice K)
Catalan.UnitModule.logLatticeAction_intertwining : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (τ : Catalan.G p K) (v : ↥(NumberField.Units.unitLattice K)),
  (Catalan.UnitLog.logRealAction K p τ) ↑v = ↑((Catalan.UnitModule.logLatticeAction p K τ) v)

  (τ : Catalan.G p K),
  (Catalan.UnitLog.logRealAction K p τ).charpoly =

    [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
      (q : ℕ) →
        Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ q →ₗ[ZMod q]
          ModN (Catalan.UnitModule.UnitLattice K) q
Catalan.UnitModule.unitReductionMap_apply : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (q : ℕ)
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
  (Catalan.UnitModule.unitReductionMap K q) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q u) =
    (ModN.mkQ q) (Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitClass K u)
Catalan.UnitModule.unitReductionMap_intertwining : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  (q : ℕ) (τ : Catalan.G p K) (v : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ q),
  (Catalan.UnitModule.unitReductionMap K q) (((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p K q) τ) v) =
    (Catalan.UnitReduction.modNMap (Catalan.UnitModule.UnitLattice K) q
        ((Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitRepresentation p K) τ))
      ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitReductionMap K q) v)
Catalan.UnitModule.logRegularEquiv : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
      [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] →
        [IsGalois ℚ K] →
          NumberField.InfinitePlace K →
            NumberField.Units.dirichletUnitTheorem.logSpace K ≃ₗ[ℝ] ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.groupZeroSum K)
Catalan.UnitModule.logRegularEquiv_intertwining : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] [inst_3 : IsGalois ℚ K] (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K) (τ : Catalan.G p K)
  (v : NumberField.Units.dirichletUnitTheorem.logSpace K),
  (Catalan.UnitModule.logRegularEquiv K w) ((Catalan.UnitLog.logRealAction K p τ) v) =
    (Catalan.UnitLog.regularZeroSumAction K τ) ((Catalan.UnitModule.logRegularEquiv K w) v)
Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnit_charpoly_regular : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] [IsGalois ℚ K] (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K) (τ : Catalan.G p K),
  (Catalan.UnitLog.regularZeroSumAction K τ).charpoly =
    Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) ((Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitRepresentation p K) τ).charpoly
Catalan.UnitModule.unitReductionMap_bijective : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ q → q ≠ 2 → Function.Bijective ⇑(Catalan.UnitModule.unitReductionMap K q)
Catalan.UnitModule.unitReductionEquiv : (p : ℕ) →
  (K : Type u_1) →
    [inst : Field K] →
      [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
        [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
          [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] →
            (q : ℕ) →
              [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
                p ≠ q →
                  q ≠ 2 →
                    Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ q ≃ₗ[ZMod q]
                      ModN (Catalan.UnitModule.UnitLattice K) q
Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation_charpoly : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K),
        ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p K q) τ).charpoly =
          Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom (ZMod q)) ((Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitRepresentation p K) τ).charpoly

Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicShift : (n : ℕ) → [NeZero n] → (ZMod n → ℝ) →ₗ[ℝ] ZMod n → ℝ
Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicShift_charpoly : ∀ (n : ℕ) [inst : NeZero n],
  (Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicShift n).charpoly = Polynomial.X ^ n - 1
Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSum : (n : ℕ) → [NeZero n] → Submodule ℝ (ZMod n → ℝ)
Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSumShift : (n : ℕ) →
  [inst : NeZero n] → ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSum n) →ₗ[ℝ] ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSum n)
Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSumShift_apply : ∀ (n : ℕ) [inst : NeZero n] (f : ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSum n))
  (i : ZMod n), ↑((Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSumShift n) f) i = ↑f (i + 1)
Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSumShift_charpoly : ∀ (n : ℕ) [inst : NeZero n],
  (Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSumShift n).charpoly = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, Polynomial.X ^ i
Catalan.UnitLog.exists_place_cycle_equiv : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] [IsGalois ℚ K]
  (τ : Gal(K/ℚ)),
  (∀ (σ : Gal(K/ℚ)), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers τ) →
    ∀ (w : NumberField.InfinitePlace K),
      ∃ e,
        e 0 = w ∧
          ∀ (i : ZMod (Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K))), e (i + 1) = (e i).comap τ.toRingEquiv.toRingHom
Catalan.UnitLog.cycleZeroSumEquiv : (K : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field K] →
    [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
      ZMod (Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K)) ≃ NumberField.InfinitePlace K →
        ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K) ≃ₗ[ℝ] ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSum (Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K)))
Catalan.UnitLog.cycleZeroSumEquiv_intertwining : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] (p : ℕ)
  (τ : Catalan.G p K) (e : ZMod (Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K)) ≃ NumberField.InfinitePlace K),
  (∀ (i : ZMod (Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K))), e (i + 1) = (e i).comap τ.toRingEquiv.toRingHom) →
    ∀ (f : ↥(Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSum K)),
      (Catalan.UnitLog.cycleZeroSumEquiv K e) ((Catalan.UnitLog.zeroSumAction K p τ) f) =
        (Catalan.UnitLog.cyclicZeroSumShift (Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K)))
          ((Catalan.UnitLog.cycleZeroSumEquiv K e) f)
Catalan.UnitLog.logRealAction_charpoly_of_generator : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [IsGalois ℚ K] (p : ℕ) (τ : Catalan.G p K),
  (∀ (σ : Catalan.G p K), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers τ) →
    (Catalan.UnitLog.logRealAction K p τ).charpoly =
      ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K)), Polynomial.X ^ i

@Catalan.UnitReduction.span_geometric_eq_norm_pair : ∀ {R : Type u_1} [inst : CommRing R] (u : R) (r : ℕ),
  IsUnit 2 → Ideal.span {∑ i ∈ Finset.range r, u ^ i} = Ideal.span {∑ i ∈ Finset.range (2 * r), u ^ i, 1 - u ^ r}
@Catalan.UnitReduction.three_step_annihilator_pairwise : ∀ {R : Type u_1} {M : Type u_2} [inst : CommRing R]
  [inst_1 : AddCommGroup M] [inst_2 : Module R M] [IsSemisimpleModule R M],
  (∃ v, Function.Surjective ⇑(LinearMap.toSpanSingleton R M v)) →
    ∀ (P W : Submodule R M),
      P ≤ W →
        Module.annihilator R (M ⧸ W) ⊔ Module.annihilator R (↥W ⧸ Submodule.comap W.subtype P) = ⊤ ∧
          Module.annihilator R (M ⧸ W) ⊔ Module.annihilator R ↥P = ⊤ ∧
            Module.annihilator R (↥W ⧸ Submodule.comap W.subtype P) ⊔ Module.annihilator R ↥P = ⊤
@Catalan.UnitReduction.three_step_annihilator_product : ∀ {R : Type u_1} {M : Type u_2} [inst : CommRing R]
  [inst_1 : AddCommGroup M] [inst_2 : Module R M] [IsSemisimpleModule R M],
  (∃ v, Function.Surjective ⇑(LinearMap.toSpanSingleton R M v)) →
    ∀ (P W : Submodule R M),
      P ≤ W →
        Module.annihilator R M =
          Module.annihilator R (M ⧸ W) * Module.annihilator R (↥W ⧸ Submodule.comap W.subtype P) *
            Module.annihilator R ↥P

  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →

      [inst : Field K] →

  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →

      [inst : Field K] →

      [inst : Field K] →

Catalan.UnitModule.unit_filtration_pairwise_of_solution : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ)
  [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ),
        x ≠ 0 →
          y ≠ 0 →
            x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
              Catalan.UnitModule.topUnitAnn p K q ⊔ Catalan.UnitModule.middleUnitAnn p K q ⋯ = ⊤ ∧
                Catalan.UnitModule.topUnitAnn p K q ⊔ Catalan.UnitModule.bottomUnitAnn p K q ⋯ = ⊤ ∧
                  Catalan.UnitModule.middleUnitAnn p K q ⋯ ⊔ Catalan.UnitModule.bottomUnitAnn p K q ⋯ = ⊤
Catalan.UnitModule.unit_filtration_product_of_solution : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ)
  [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ),
        x ≠ 0 →
          y ≠ 0 →
            x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
              Catalan.UnitModule.topUnitAnn p K q * Catalan.UnitModule.middleUnitAnn p K q ⋯ *
                  Catalan.UnitModule.bottomUnitAnn p K q ⋯ =
                Catalan.UnitModule.normPlusIdeal p K q
Catalan.UnitModule.middleUnitAnn_ne_top : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  q < p → Catalan.UnitModule.middleUnitAnn p K q ⋯ ≠ ⊤
Catalan.UnitModule.iota_square : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K], Catalan.ι p K ^ 2 = 1
Catalan.UnitModule.plusAugIdeal : (p : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] →
    (K : Type u_1) →
      [inst : Field K] →
        [inst_1 : NumberField K] →
          [IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] → (q : ℕ) → Ideal (MonoidAlgebra (ZMod q) (Catalan.G p K))
Catalan.UnitModule.plusAugIdeal_annihilator : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  q ≠ 2 →
    ¬q ∣ Nat.card (Catalan.G p K) →
      Submodule.annihilator (Catalan.UnitModule.plusAugIdeal p K q) = Catalan.UnitModule.normPlusIdeal p K q
Catalan.UnitModule.plusAugIdeal_annihilator_of_solution : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ),
        x ≠ 0 →
          y ≠ 0 →
            x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
              Submodule.annihilator (Catalan.UnitModule.plusAugIdeal p K q) = Catalan.UnitModule.normPlusIdeal p K q
Catalan.UnitModule.plusAug_unit_annihilator_of_solution : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)
  [inst_1 : Field K] [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ)
  [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (x y : ℤ),
        x ≠ 0 →
          y ≠ 0 →
            x ^ p = y ^ q + 1 →
              Submodule.annihilator (Catalan.UnitModule.plusAugIdeal p K q) =
                Module.annihilator (MonoidAlgebra (ZMod q) (Catalan.G p K)) (Catalan.UnitModule.UnitPowerModule p K q)

Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation_charpoly_of_torsion_le : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K]
  [inst_1 : NumberField K] (q : ℕ) [inst_2 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  NumberField.Units.torsion K ≤ Catalan.UnitQuotient.qPowers (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ q →
    ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K),
      ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p K q) τ).charpoly =
        Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom (ZMod q)) ((Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitRepresentation p K) τ).charpoly

  [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] (q : ℕ), Odd q → Function.Injective fun u => u ^ q

@Catalan.UnitReduction.dual_charpoly_eq : ∀ {k : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} [inst : Field k] [inst_1 : AddCommGroup V]
  [inst_2 : Module k V] [inst_3 : FiniteDimensional k V] (T : V →ₗ[k] V),
  (Module.Dual.transpose T).charpoly = T.charpoly
@Catalan.UnitReduction.representation_dual_charpoly : ∀ {k : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {V : Type u_3} [inst : Field k]
  [inst_1 : Group G] [inst_2 : AddCommGroup V] [inst_3 : Module k V] [inst_4 : FiniteDimensional k V]
  (ρ : Representation k G V) (g : G), (ρ.dual g).charpoly = (ρ g⁻¹).charpoly
@Catalan.UnitReduction.cyclic_geometric_projective_rigidity : ∀ {F : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} [inst : Field F]
  [inst_1 : AddCommGroup V] [inst_2 : Module F V] (T : V →ₗ[F] V) (n : ℕ),
  2 < n →
    minpoly F T = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, Polynomial.X ^ i →
      ∀ (v : V),
        Function.Surjective ⇑(LinearMap.toSpanSingleton (Polynomial F) (Module.AEval' T) ((Module.AEval'.of T) v)) →
          ∀ k < n, ∀ (a : F), (T ^ k) v = a • v → k = 0 ∧ a = 1

  [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] (q : ℕ), Odd q → Function.Bijective ⇑(Catalan.UnitModule.unitReductionMap K q)
Catalan.UnitModule.real_unitRepresentation_charpoly : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] (q : ℕ) [inst_3 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  Odd q →
    ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K),
      ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p K q) τ).charpoly =
        Polynomial.map (Int.castRingHom (ZMod q)) ((Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitRepresentation p K) τ).charpoly
Catalan.UnitModule.real_unit_charpoly_of_generator : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] [IsGalois ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  Odd q →
    ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K),
      (∀ (σ : Catalan.G p K), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers τ) →
        ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p K q) τ).charpoly =
          ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K)), Polynomial.X ^ i
Catalan.UnitModule.real_unit_minpoly_of_generator : ∀ (p : ℕ) (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K]
  [NumberField.IsTotallyReal K] [IsGalois ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [inst_4 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  Odd q →
    ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p K),
      (∀ (σ : Catalan.G p K), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers τ) →
        ¬q ∣ Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K) →
          minpoly (ZMod q) ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p K q) τ) =
            ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace K)), Polynomial.X ^ i
Catalan.UnitQuotient.field_qth_root_of_coprime_degree : ∀ (F : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst : Field F]
  [inst_1 : Field L] [inst_2 : Algebra F L] [FiniteDimensional F L] (q : ℕ),
  q.Coprime (Module.finrank F L) → ∀ (a : F), (∃ b, b ^ q = (algebraMap F L) a) → ∃ b, b ^ q = a
Catalan.UnitQuotient.unit_qth_root_of_field_qth_root : ∀ (F : Type u_1) [inst : Field F] [NumberField F] (q : ℕ),
  0 < q → ∀ (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers F)ˣ), (∃ b, b ^ q = ↑↑u) → ∃ v, v ^ q = u
Catalan.A3.F_infinitePlace_card : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)],
  p ≠ 2 → Fintype.card (NumberField.InfinitePlace (Catalan.A3.F p)) = (p - 1) / 2
Catalan.A3.F_unit_charpoly : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
        (∀ (σ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers τ) →
          ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q) τ).charpoly =
            ∑ i ∈ Finset.range ((p - 1) / 2), Polynomial.X ^ i
Catalan.A3.F_unit_dual_charpoly : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
        (∀ (σ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers τ) →
          ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual τ).charpoly =
            ∑ i ∈ Finset.range ((p - 1) / 2), Polynomial.X ^ i
Catalan.A3.F_unit_dual_cyclic : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ¬q ∣ (p - 1) / 2 →
        ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
          (∀ (σ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers τ) →
            ∃ v,
              Function.Surjective
                ⇑(LinearMap.toSpanSingleton (Polynomial (ZMod q))
                    (Module.AEval' ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual τ)) v)
Catalan.A3.F_unit_dual_minpoly : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ¬q ∣ (p - 1) / 2 →
        ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
          (∀ (σ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers τ) →
            minpoly (ZMod q) ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual τ) =
              ∑ i ∈ Finset.range ((p - 1) / 2), Polynomial.X ^ i
Catalan.A3.F_unit_dual_projective_generator : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  7 ≤ p →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ¬q ∣ (p - 1) / 2 →
        ∀ (τ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
          (∀ (σ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers τ) →
            ∃ v,
              v ≠ 0 ∧
                Function.Surjective
                    ⇑(LinearMap.toSpanSingleton (Polynomial (ZMod q))
                        (Module.AEval' ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual τ))
                        ((Module.AEval'.of ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual τ)) v)) ∧
                  ∀ k < (p - 1) / 2,
                    ∀ (a : ZMod q),
                      ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual τ ^ k) v = a • v →
                        k = 0 ∧ a = 1

Catalan.Kummer.aut_fixed_of_pow_eq_one : ∀ (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : Field L]
  [inst_2 : Algebra K L] (n : ℕ) [NeZero n] (ζ : K), IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n → ∀ (σ : Gal(L/K)) (a : L), a ^ n = 1 → σ a = a
Catalan.Kummer.root_ratio_independent : ∀ (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : Field L]
  [inst_2 : Algebra K L] (n : ℕ) [NeZero n] (ζ : K),
  IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n → ∀ (σ : Gal(L/K)) (r s : L), r ≠ 0 → s ≠ 0 → r ^ n = s ^ n → σ r / r = σ s / s
Catalan.Kummer.root_ratio_aut_mul : ∀ (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : Field L]
  [inst_2 : Algebra K L] (n : ℕ) [NeZero n] (ζ : K),
  IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n →
    ∀ (σ τ : Gal(L/K)) (r : L), r ≠ 0 → ∀ (a : K), r ^ n = (algebraMap K L) a → (σ * τ) r / r = σ r / r * (τ r / r)
Catalan.Kummer.rootsOfUnityCoordinate : (L : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field L] → (n : ℕ) → [NeZero n] → (ζ : Lˣ) → IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n → Additive ↥(rootsOfUnity n L) ≃+ ZMod n
Catalan.Kummer.rootsOfUnityCoordinate_symm_nat : ∀ (L : Type u_1) [inst : Field L] (n : ℕ) [inst_1 : NeZero n] (ζ : Lˣ)
  (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n) (i : ℕ),
  ↑(Additive.toMul ((Catalan.Kummer.rootsOfUnityCoordinate L n ζ hζ).symm ↑i)) = ζ ^ i

Catalan.A3.kummerZeta_spec : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → IsPrimitiveRoot (Catalan.A3.kummerZeta p q) q
Catalan.A3.kummerZetaM : (p q : ℕ) → ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)
Catalan.A3.kummerZetaM_spec : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → IsPrimitiveRoot (Catalan.A3.kummerZetaM p q) q
Catalan.A3.exists_unitRoot_M : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  0 < q →
    ∀ (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
      ∃ r, r ^ q = (algebraMap (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) ↑↑u
Catalan.A3.unitRoot : (p q : ℕ) → 0 < q → (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ → ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)
Catalan.A3.unitRoot_pow : ∀ (p q : ℕ) (hq : 0 < q) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q hq u ^ q = (algebraMap (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) ↑↑u
Catalan.A3.unitRoot_ne_zero : ∀ (p q : ℕ) (hq : 0 < q) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q hq u ≠ 0
Catalan.A3.unitRoots_algHom_ext : ∀ (p q : ℕ) (hq : 0 < q) (L : Type u_1) [inst : Field L]
  [inst_1 : Algebra (Catalan.A3.F p) L] (φ ψ : ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q) →ₐ[Catalan.A3.F p] L),
  φ (Catalan.A3.kummerZetaM p q) = ψ (Catalan.A3.kummerZetaM p q) →
    (∀ (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
        φ (Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q hq u) = ψ (Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q hq u)) →
      φ = ψ
Catalan.A3.instAlgebraBsubMsub : (p q : ℕ) → Algebra ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)
Catalan.A3.instModuleBsubMsub : (p q : ℕ) → Module ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)
Catalan.A3.instScalarTowerFBsubMsub : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  IsScalarTower (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)
Catalan.A3.instScalarTowerBsubMsubOmega : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  IsScalarTower (↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)) (↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) Catalan.A3.Omega
Catalan.A3.algebraMap_Bsub_Msub_coe : ∀ (p q : ℕ) (b : ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)),
  ↑((algebraMap ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) b) = ↑b
Catalan.A3.finiteDimensional_Msub_over_B : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  0 < q → FiniteDimensional ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)
Catalan.A3.isGalois_Msub_over_B : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → IsGalois ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)
Catalan.A3.kummerValue : (p q : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
    Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)) →
      (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ → ↥(rootsOfUnity q ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q))
Catalan.A3.kummerValue_val : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  ↑↑(Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q σ u) = σ (Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q ⋯ u) / Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q ⋯ u
Catalan.A3.kummerValue_eq_one_iff : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q σ u = 1 ↔ σ (Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q ⋯ u) = Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q ⋯ u
Catalan.A3.kummerValue_mul_left : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ τ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q (σ * τ) u = Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q σ u * Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q τ u
Catalan.A3.kummerValue_mul_right : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))) (u v : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q σ (u * v) = Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q σ u * Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q σ v
Catalan.A3.kummerValue_one_right : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))), Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q σ 1 = 1
Catalan.A3.kummerValue_one_left : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ), Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q 1 u = 1
Catalan.A3.kummerPairingHom : (p q : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
    Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)) →*
      (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ →* ↥(rootsOfUnity q ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q))
Catalan.A3.kummerPairingHom_injective : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  Function.Injective ⇑(Catalan.A3.kummerPairingHom p q)
Catalan.A3.unit_qth_root_of_all_aut_fix_unitRoot : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  (∀ (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))),
      σ (Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q ⋯ u) = Catalan.A3.unitRoot p q ⋯ u) →
    ∃ v, v ^ q = u
Catalan.A3.kummerZetaUnitM : (p q : ℕ) → [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] → (↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q))ˣ
Catalan.A3.kummerZetaUnitM_spec : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  IsPrimitiveRoot (Catalan.A3.kummerZetaUnitM p q) q
Catalan.A3.kummerCoordinate : (p q : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] → Additive ↥(rootsOfUnity q ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) ≃+ ZMod q
Catalan.A3.kummerQuotientValue : (p q : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
    Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)) →
      (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ ⧸
          Catalan.UnitQuotient.qPowers (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q →*
        ↥(rootsOfUnity q ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q))
Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional : (p q : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
    Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)) →
      Module.Dual (ZMod q) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q)
Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional_apply : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q σ) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q u) =
    (Catalan.A3.kummerCoordinate p q) (Additive.ofMul (Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q σ u))
Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional_apply_eq_zero_iff : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))) (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q σ) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q u) = 0 ↔ Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q σ u = 1
Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional_one : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)], Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q 1 = 0
Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional_mul : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ τ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))),
  Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q (σ * τ) = Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q σ + Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q τ
Catalan.A3.kummerDualHom : (p q : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
    Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)) →*
      Multiplicative
        (Module.Dual (ZMod q) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q))
Catalan.A3.kummerDualHom_injective : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  Function.Injective ⇑(Catalan.A3.kummerDualHom p q)
Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional_right_nondegenerate : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (z : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q),
  (∀ (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))), (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q σ) z = 0) → z = 0
Catalan.A3.kummerGalCommGroup : (p q : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] → CommGroup Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))
Catalan.A3.kummerGal_pow_eq_one : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))), σ ^ q = 1
Catalan.A3.kummerGalModule : (p q : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] → Module (ZMod q) (Additive Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)))
Catalan.A3.kummerPairing : (p q : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
    Additive Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)) →ₗ[ZMod q]
      Module.Dual (ZMod q) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q)
Catalan.A3.kummerPairing_apply : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)))
  (z : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q),
  ((Catalan.A3.kummerPairing p q) (Additive.ofMul σ)) z = (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q σ) z
Catalan.A3.kummerPairing_isPerfect : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)], (Catalan.A3.kummerPairing p q).IsPerfPair
Catalan.A3.kummerDualEquiv : (p q : ℕ) →
  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
    Additive Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)) ≃ₗ[ZMod q]
      Module.Dual (ZMod q) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q)
Catalan.A3.kummerDualEquiv_apply : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)))
  (z : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q),
  ((Catalan.A3.kummerDualEquiv p q) (Additive.ofMul σ)) z = (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q σ) z
Catalan.A3.exists_kummer_aut_of_functional : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]
  (f : Module.Dual (ZMod q) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q)),
  ∃ σ, Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q σ = f
