Catalan.Kummer.rootsOfUnityCoordinate_aut : ∀ (L : Type u_1) [inst : Field L] (n : ℕ) [inst_1 : NeZero n] (ζ : Lˣ)
  (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n) (σ : L ≃+* L) (u : ↥(rootsOfUnity n L)),
  (Catalan.Kummer.rootsOfUnityCoordinate L n ζ hζ) (Additive.ofMul ((σ.toMulEquiv.restrictRootsOfUnity n) u)) =
    (Catalan.Kummer.rootsOfUnityCoordinate L n ζ hζ)
        (Additive.ofMul
          ((σ.toMulEquiv.restrictRootsOfUnity n)
            (Additive.toMul ((Catalan.Kummer.rootsOfUnityCoordinate L n ζ hζ).symm 1)))) *
      (Catalan.Kummer.rootsOfUnityCoordinate L n ζ hζ) (Additive.ofMul u)
Catalan.Kummer.conjugateOver : (k : Type u_1) →
  (B : Type u_2) →
    (L : Type u_3) →
      [inst : Field k] →
        [inst_1 : Field B] →
          [inst_2 : Field L] →
            [inst_3 : Algebra k B] →
              [inst_4 : Algebra k L] →
                [inst_5 : Algebra B L] → [IsScalarTower k B L] → [Normal k B] → Gal(L/k) → Gal(L/B) → Gal(L/B)
Catalan.Kummer.conjugateOver_apply : ∀ (k : Type u_1) (B : Type u_2) (L : Type u_3) [inst : Field k] [inst_1 : Field B]
  [inst_2 : Field L] [inst_3 : Algebra k B] [inst_4 : Algebra k L] [inst_5 : Algebra B L] [inst_6 : IsScalarTower k B L]
  [inst_7 : Normal k B] (σ : Gal(L/k)) (τ : Gal(L/B)) (x : L),
  (Catalan.Kummer.conjugateOver k B L σ τ) x = σ (τ (σ⁻¹ x))
Catalan.Kummer.conjugateOver_restrictScalars : ∀ (k : Type u_1) (B : Type u_2) (L : Type u_3) [inst : Field k]
  [inst_1 : Field B] [inst_2 : Field L] [inst_3 : Algebra k B] [inst_4 : Algebra k L] [inst_5 : Algebra B L]
  [inst_6 : IsScalarTower k B L] [inst_7 : Normal k B] (σ : Gal(L/k)) (τ : Gal(L/B)),
  AlgEquiv.restrictScalars k (Catalan.Kummer.conjugateOver k B L σ τ) = σ * AlgEquiv.restrictScalars k τ * σ⁻¹
Catalan.Kummer.conjugateOver_eq_self_of_commute : ∀ (k : Type u_1) (B : Type u_2) (L : Type u_3) [inst : Field k]
  [inst_1 : Field B] [inst_2 : Field L] [inst_3 : Algebra k B] [inst_4 : Algebra k L] [inst_5 : Algebra B L]
  [inst_6 : IsScalarTower k B L] [inst_7 : Normal k B] (σ : Gal(L/k)) (τ : Gal(L/B)),
  σ * AlgEquiv.restrictScalars k τ = AlgEquiv.restrictScalars k τ * σ → Catalan.Kummer.conjugateOver k B L σ τ = τ
Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar : (L : Type u_1) →
  [inst : Field L] → (n : ℕ) → [NeZero n] → (ζ : Lˣ) → IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n → L ≃+* L → ZMod n
Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar_one : ∀ (L : Type u_1) [inst : Field L] (n : ℕ) [inst_1 : NeZero n] (ζ : Lˣ)
  (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n), Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar L n ζ hζ (RingEquiv.refl L) = 1
Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar_trans : ∀ (L : Type u_1) [inst : Field L] (n : ℕ) [inst_1 : NeZero n] (ζ : Lˣ)
  (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n) (σ τ : L ≃+* L),
  Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar L n ζ hζ (σ.trans τ) =
    Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar L n ζ hζ σ * Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar L n ζ hζ τ
Catalan.Kummer.isUnit_rootCoordinateScalar : ∀ (L : Type u_1) [inst : Field L] (n : ℕ) [inst_1 : NeZero n] (ζ : Lˣ)
  (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n) (σ : L ≃+* L), IsUnit (Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar L n ζ hζ σ)
Catalan.A3.inertiaTrivial_iff_isUnramifiedAt : ∀ (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst : Field K] [NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : Field L] [NumberField L] [inst_4 : Algebra K L] [IsGalois K L] (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L))
  [inst_6 : P.IsMaximal],
  P ≠ ⊥ → (Catalan.A3.InertiaTrivial K L P ↔ Algebra.IsUnramifiedAt (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) P)
Catalan.A3.restrictToF : (p q : ℕ) → 0 < p → Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ) → Gal(Catalan.A3.F p/ℚ)
Catalan.A3.restrictToF_commutes : ∀ (p q : ℕ) (hp : 0 < p) (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)) (a : Catalan.A3.F p),
  (algebraMap (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) ((Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ) a) =
    σ ((algebraMap (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) a)
Catalan.A3.restrictToF_inv : ∀ (p q : ℕ) (hp : 0 < p) (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
  Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ⁻¹ = (Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ)⁻¹
Catalan.A3.restrictToF_unitAction : ∀ (p q : ℕ) (hp : 0 < p) (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ))
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  σ ((algebraMap (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) ↑↑u) =
    (algebraMap (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q))
      ↑↑((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p (Catalan.A3.F p) (Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ)) u)
Catalan.A3.conjugateKummer : (p q : ℕ) →
  [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] →
    0 < p →
      Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ) →
        Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)) → Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))
Catalan.A3.conjugateKummer_apply : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp : 0 < p)
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)) (τ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)))
  (x : ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)), (Catalan.A3.conjugateKummer p q hp σ τ) x = σ (τ (σ⁻¹ x))
Catalan.A3.conjugateKummer_eq_self_of_commute : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp : 0 < p)
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)) (τ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))),
  σ * AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ τ = AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ τ * σ → Catalan.A3.conjugateKummer p q hp σ τ = τ
Catalan.A3.isUnramifiedAtInfinitePlaces_of_odd_exponent : ∀ (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst : Field K]
  [NumberField K] [inst_2 : Field L] [NumberField L] [inst_4 : Algebra K L] [IsGalois K L] (q : ℕ),
  Odd q → (∀ (σ : Gal(L/K)), σ ^ q = 1) → IsUnramifiedAtInfinitePlaces K L
Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar : (p q : ℕ) → [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] → Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ) → ZMod q
Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar_isUnit : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
  IsUnit (Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar p q σ)
Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar_ne_zero : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
  Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar p q σ ≠ 0
Catalan.A3.kummerValue_conjugate : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp : 0 < p)
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)) (τ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)))
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
  Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q (Catalan.A3.conjugateKummer p q hp σ τ) u =
    (σ.toRingEquiv.toMulEquiv.restrictRootsOfUnity q)
      (Catalan.A3.kummerValue p q τ
        ((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p (Catalan.A3.F p) (Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ)⁻¹) u))
Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional_conjugate_apply : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp : 0 < p)
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)) (τ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)))
  (z : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ q),
  (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q (Catalan.A3.conjugateKummer p q hp σ τ)) z =
    Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar p q σ *
      (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q τ)
        (((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q) (Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ)⁻¹) z)
Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional_conjugate : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp : 0 < p)
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)) (τ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))),
  Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q (Catalan.A3.conjugateKummer p q hp σ τ) =
    Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar p q σ •
      ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual (Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ))
        (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q τ)
Catalan.A3.commuting_kummerFunctional_eigen : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp : 0 < p)
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)) (τ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))),
  σ * AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ τ = AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ τ * σ →
    ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual (Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ))
        (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q τ) =
      (Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar p q σ)⁻¹ • Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q τ
Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar_eq_one_iff : ∀ (L : Type u_1) [inst : Field L] (n : ℕ) [inst_1 : NeZero n] (ζ : Lˣ)
  (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ n) (σ : L ≃+* L), Catalan.Kummer.rootCoordinateScalar L n ζ hζ σ = 1 ↔ σ ↑ζ = ↑ζ
Catalan.A3.unramifiedAbelianQ_finitePlaces : ∀ (p q : ℕ) (J : IntermediateField (Catalan.A3.F p) Catalan.A3.Omega),
  Catalan.A3.UnramifiedAbelianQ p q J →
    ∀ (P : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers ↥J)),
      Algebra.IsUnramifiedAt (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p)) P.asIdeal
Catalan.A3.unramifiedAbelianQ_infinitePlaces : ∀ (p q : ℕ),
  Odd q →
    ∀ (J : IntermediateField (Catalan.A3.F p) Catalan.A3.Omega),
      Catalan.A3.UnramifiedAbelianQ p q J → IsUnramifiedAtInfinitePlaces (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥J
Catalan.A3.unramifiedAbelianQ_of_unramifiedAtFinitePlaces : ∀ (p q : ℕ)
  (J : IntermediateField (Catalan.A3.F p) Catalan.A3.Omega),
  FiniteDimensional (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥J →
    IsGalois (Catalan.A3.F p) ↥J →
      (∀ (σ τ : Gal(↥J/Catalan.A3.F p)), σ * τ = τ * σ) →
        (∀ (σ : Gal(↥J/Catalan.A3.F p)), σ ^ q = 1) →
          (∀ (P : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers ↥J)),
              Algebra.IsUnramifiedAt (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p)) P.asIdeal) →
            Catalan.A3.UnramifiedAbelianQ p q J
Catalan.A3.fixes_B_of_restrictToF_eq_one_of_zeta_fixed : ∀ (p q : ℕ) (hp : 0 < p) (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
  Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ = 1 →
    σ (Catalan.A3.kummerZetaM p q) = Catalan.A3.kummerZetaM p q →
      ∀ (b : ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)),
        σ ((algebraMap ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) b) =
          (algebraMap ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) b
Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar_eq_one_iff : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
  Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar p q σ = 1 ↔ σ (Catalan.A3.kummerZetaM p q) = Catalan.A3.kummerZetaM p q
Catalan.A3.abs_pow_eq_one_of_restrictToF_eq_one_of_scalar_eq_one : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp : 0 < p)
  (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
  Catalan.A3.restrictToF p q hp σ = 1 → Catalan.A3.cyclotomicScalar p q σ = 1 → σ ^ q = 1
Catalan.A3.exists_kummer_centralizer_element : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  7 ≤ p →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ¬q ∣ (p - 1) / 2 →
        ∃ τ,
          τ ≠ 1 ∧
            τ ^ q = 1 ∧
              ∀ (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
                σ * AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ τ = AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ τ * σ →
                  (∀ (b : ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)),
                      σ ((algebraMap ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) b) =
                        (algebraMap ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) b) ∧
                    σ ^ q = 1

Catalan.A3.galEquivOfFieldEquivs : (K : Type u_1) →
  (K' : Type u_2) →
    (L : Type u_3) →
      (L' : Type u_4) →
        [inst : Field K] →
          [inst_1 : Field K'] →
            [inst_2 : Field L] →
              [inst_3 : Field L'] →
                [inst_4 : Algebra K L] →
                  [inst_5 : Algebra K' L'] →
                    (f : K ≃+* K') →
                      (e : L ≃+* L') →
                        (∀ (x : K), e ((algebraMap K L) x) = (algebraMap K' L') (f x)) → Gal(L/K) ≃* Gal(L'/K')

  [inst_5 : Algebra K' L'] (f : K ≃+* K') (e : L ≃+* L')
  (hcompat : ∀ (x : K), e ((algebraMap K L) x) = (algebraMap K' L') (f x)) (σ : Gal(L/K)) (x : L'),

Catalan.Runge.map_rungeCoeff_eq_binomialProductCoeff : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1)

  (τ : K →+* ℂ) (k : ℕ),
  τ (Catalan.Runge.rungeCoeff p K q Theta k) =
    Catalan.Runge.binomialProductCoeff (Catalan.G p K) (fun g => ↑(Theta.coeff g) / ↑q)
      (fun g => -τ (g (Catalan.ζ p K))) k

Catalan.Runge.root_eq_scaled_rungeFunction : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
  p ≠ q →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ∀ (x : ℤ),
        1 < |↑x| →
          ∀ (Theta : Catalan.R p K),
            (∀ (g : Catalan.G p K), 0 ≤ Theta.coeff g) →
              Catalan.Runge.EvenCoefficients p K Theta →
                ∀ (m : ℕ),
                  Catalan.weight p K Theta = ↑(m * q) →
                    ∀ (u : K),
                      u ^ q = ↑(Catalan.upow p K (Catalan.xmζ p K x hp2) Theta) →
                        ∀ (τ : K →+* ℂ), τ u = ↑x ^ m * Catalan.Runge.rungeFunction p K q Theta τ (↑x)⁻¹
'Catalan.Runge.root_eq_scaled_rungeFunction' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Catalan.Runge.multichoose_tail_le : ∀ (m j : ℕ),
  0 < m → m.multichoose (m + 1 + j) ≤ (2 * m).choose (m + 1) * (2 * m + 1).multichoose j
'Catalan.Runge.multichoose_tail_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Catalan.Runge.norm_series_tail_le : ∀ (m : ℕ),
  0 < m →
    ∀ (c : ℕ → ℂ) (z v : ℂ),
      ‖z‖ < 1 →
        (∀ (k : ℕ), ‖c k‖ ≤ ↑(m.multichoose k)) →
          HasSum (fun k => c k * z ^ k) v →
            ‖v - ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (m + 1), c k * z ^ k‖ ≤
              ↑((2 * m).choose (m + 1)) * ‖z‖ ^ (m + 1) / (1 - ‖z‖) ^ (2 * m + 1)
'Catalan.Runge.norm_series_tail_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  q ≠ 0 →

Catalan.Runge.runge_estimate : ∀ (p : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] (K : Type u_1) [inst_1 : Field K]
  [inst_2 : NumberField K] [inst_3 : IsCyclotomicExtension {p} ℚ K] (q : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    7 ≤ q →
      ∀ (hp2 : p ≠ 2),
        p ≠ q →
          ∀ (x : ℤ),
            1 < |↑x| →
              ∀ (Theta : Catalan.R p K),
                (∀ (g : Catalan.G p K), 0 ≤ Theta.coeff g) →
                  Catalan.Runge.EvenCoefficients p K Theta →
                    ∀ (m : ℕ),
                      0 < m →
                        Catalan.weight p K Theta = ↑(m * q) →
                          ∀ (u : K),

                              ∀ (τ : K →+* ℂ),
                                ‖τ (↑q ^ Catalan.Runge.D q m * u - Catalan.Runge.rungeApprox p K q Theta m x)‖ ≤
                                  Catalan.Runge.errorBound q m |↑x|

Catalan.A3.exists_cyclic_kummer_centralizer_element : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  7 ≤ p →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ¬q ∣ (p - 1) / 2 →
        ∃ γ τ,
          (∀ (σ : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)), σ ∈ Subgroup.zpowers γ) ∧
            τ ≠ 1 ∧
              τ ^ q = 1 ∧
                Function.Surjective
                    ⇑(LinearMap.toSpanSingleton (Polynomial (ZMod q))
                        (Module.AEval' ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual γ))
                        ((Module.AEval'.of ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual γ))
                          (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q τ))) ∧
                  ∀ (σ : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
                    σ * AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ τ = AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ τ * σ →
                      (∀ (b : ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)),
                          σ ((algebraMap ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) b) =
                            (algebraMap ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) b) ∧
                        σ ^ q = 1
'Catalan.A3.exists_cyclic_kummer_centralizer_element' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

Catalan.A3.selector_of_T_lift : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  0 < p →
    ∀ (tauM : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))),
      tauM ≠ 1 →
        (∀ (rho : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
            rho * AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ tauM = AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ tauM * rho →
              ∀ (b : ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q)),
                rho ((algebraMap ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) b) =
                  (algebraMap ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q) ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) b) →
          ∀ (sigmaB : Gal(Catalan.A3.T p q/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))),
            (∀ (x : ↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)),
                sigmaB ((algebraMap (↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) (Catalan.A3.T p q)) x) =
                  (algebraMap (↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)) (Catalan.A3.T p q)) (tauM x)) →
              Catalan.A3.Selector p q (AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ sigmaB)
'Catalan.A3.selector_of_T_lift' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

Catalan.A3.relativeFrob_congruence_of_rationalFrob_compatible : ∀ (K : Type u_1) (E : Type u_2) (L : Type u_3)
  [inst : Field K] [NumberField K] [inst_2 : Field E] [NumberField E] [inst_4 : Field L] [inst_5 : NumberField L]
  [inst_6 : Algebra K E] [inst_7 : Algebra K L] [inst_8 : Algebra E L] [IsScalarTower K E L] (ell : ℕ)
  (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) (sigma : Gal(L/ℚ)) (tau : Gal(E/K)),
  Catalan.A3.IsArithmeticFrob ell P sigma →
    (∀ (x : E), sigma ((algebraMap E L) x) = (algebraMap E L) (tau x)) →
      Nat.card
            (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K ⧸
              Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers E) P)) =
          ell →
        ∀ (x : NumberField.RingOfIntegers E),
          (Catalan.A3.integralAut tau) x -
              x ^
                Nat.card
                  (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K ⧸
                    Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers E) P)) ∈
            Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers E) P

 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]

Catalan.A3.classGroupToHGal_prime_zpowers_of_rationalFrob : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hq : Odd q)
  (sigmaB : Gal(Catalan.A3.T p q/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))) (ell : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime ell →
    ∀ (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.T p q))),
      Catalan.A3.IsArithmeticFrob ell P (AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ sigmaB) →
        ∀ (v : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))),
          v.asIdeal = Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p)) P →
            Subgroup.zpowers
                ((Catalan.A3.classGroupToHGal p q hq)
                  ((ClassGroup.mk (Catalan.A3.F p)) (FractionalIdealGroup.prime v))) =
              Subgroup.zpowers ((Catalan.A3.restrictTToH p q) sigmaB)

Catalan.A3.exists_H_prime_class_of_rationalFrob : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hq : Odd q)
  (sigmaB : Gal(Catalan.A3.T p q/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))) (ell : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime ell →
    ∀ (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.T p q))),
      Catalan.A3.IsArithmeticFrob ell P (AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ sigmaB) →
        ∃ v,
          v.asIdeal = Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p)) P ∧
            Nat.card (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p) ⧸ v.asIdeal) = ell ∧
              Subgroup.zpowers
                  ((Catalan.A3.classGroupToHGal p q hq)
                    ((ClassGroup.mk (Catalan.A3.F p)) (FractionalIdealGroup.prime v))) =
                Subgroup.zpowers ((Catalan.A3.restrictTToH p q) sigmaB)

Catalan.A3.exists_prime_class_of_selector_avoiding : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  0 < p →
    ∀ (hq : Odd q) (sigmaB : Gal(Catalan.A3.T p q/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))),
      Catalan.A3.Selector p q (AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ sigmaB) →
        ∀ (S : Finset ℕ),
          ∃ ell,
            Nat.Prime ell ∧
              ell ∉ S ∧
                ∃ P a v,
                  0 < a ∧
                    a < q ∧
                      Catalan.A3.IsArithmeticFrob ell P (AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ sigmaB ^ a) ∧
                        v.asIdeal = Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p)) P ∧
                          Nat.card (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p) ⧸ v.asIdeal) = ell ∧
                            Subgroup.zpowers
                                ((Catalan.A3.classGroupToHGal p q hq)
                                  ((ClassGroup.mk (Catalan.A3.F p)) (FractionalIdealGroup.prime v))) =
                              Subgroup.zpowers ((Catalan.A3.restrictTToH p q) sigmaB)

Catalan.Kummer.integral_root_fixed_of_residue_power : ∀ (K : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) [inst : Field K] [NumberField K]
  [inst_2 : Field L] [NumberField L] [inst_4 : Algebra K L] (q ell : ℕ),
  Nat.Prime q →
    Nat.Prime ell →
      q ≠ ell →
        ∀ (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)),
          P.IsMaximal →
            ↑ell ∈ P →
              Nat.card (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K ⧸ Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) P) = ell →
                ∀ (sigma : Gal(L/K)),
                  (∀ (x : NumberField.RingOfIntegers L), (Catalan.A3.integralAut sigma) x - x ^ ell ∈ P) →
                    ∀ (zeta : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ),
                      IsPrimitiveRoot (↑zeta) q →
                        ∀ (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K)ˣ) (r : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)ˣ),
                          r ^ q =
                              (Units.map ↑(algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)))
                                u →
                            (∃ v,
                                v ^ q =
                                  (Units.map ↑(Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers K) P))) u) →
                              (Catalan.A3.integralAut sigma) ↑r = ↑r

Catalan.A3.unit_qth_power_of_cyclic_prime_residues : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)] (hp : 0 < p),
  Odd q →
    ∀ (gamma : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)) (tau : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))),
      Function.Surjective
          ⇑(LinearMap.toSpanSingleton (Polynomial (ZMod q))
              (Module.AEval' ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual gamma))
              ((Module.AEval'.of ((Catalan.UnitModule.unitRepresentation p (Catalan.A3.F p) q).dual gamma))
                (Catalan.A3.kummerFunctional p q tau))) →
        ∀ (sigmaB : Gal(Catalan.A3.T p q/↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p q))) (rho : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Msub p q)/ℚ)),
          (Catalan.A3.restrictTToM p q) sigmaB = Catalan.A3.conjugateKummer p q hp rho tau →
            ∀ (ell : ℕ),
              Nat.Prime ell →
                q ≠ ell →
                  ∀ (P : Ideal (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.T p q))) (a : ℕ),
                    0 < a →
                      a < q →
                        Catalan.A3.IsArithmeticFrob ell P (AlgEquiv.restrictScalars ℚ sigmaB ^ a) →
                          ∀ (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
                            (∀ (g : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
                                ∃ v,
                                  v ^ q =
                                    (Units.map
                                        ↑(Ideal.Quotient.mk
                                            (Ideal.under (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p)) P)))
                                      ((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p (Catalan.A3.F p) g) u)) →
                              ∃ v, v ^ q = u

            (∀ (g : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
                ∃ w, w ^ q = (Units.map ↑red) ((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p (Catalan.A3.F p) g) u)) →
              ∃ w, w ^ q = u) →
          (Catalan.A3.unitResidueCoordinates p q ell red e).range =
            Catalan.Residue.sumZero (ZMod q) (Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p))
'Catalan.A3.unitResidueCoordinates_range' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Catalan.A3.exists_unit_augmentation_coordinates : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime q)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ¬q ∣ (p - 1) / 2 →
        ∀ (ell : ℕ) (red : NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p) →+* ZMod ell)
          (e : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (ZMod ell)ˣ q ≃ₗ[ZMod q] ZMod q),
          (∀ (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ),
              (∀ (g : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
                  ∃ w, w ^ q = (Units.map ↑red) ((Catalan.Circular.unitAction p (Catalan.A3.F p) g) u)) →
                ∃ w, w ^ q = u) →
            ∃ u,
              ∀ (g : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
                (Catalan.A3.unitResidueCoordinates p q ell red e) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q u) g =

@Catalan.Residue.inverseOrbitMap_algebra_action : ∀ {k : Type u_1} {G : Type u_2} {V : Type u_3} [inst : Field k]
  [inst_1 : Group G] [inst_2 : Fintype G] [inst_3 : AddCommGroup V] [inst_4 : Module k V] (pi : Representation k G V)

  (Catalan.Residue.inverseOrbitMap pi f) ((pi.asAlgebraHom Theta) z) g =

  (f : V →ₗ[k] k) (z : V) (b : k),

  (red : NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p) →+* ZMod ell)
  (e : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (ZMod ell)ˣ q ≃ₗ[ZMod q] ZMod q)

  (red : NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p) →+* ZMod ell)
  (e : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (ZMod ell)ˣ q ≃ₗ[ZMod q] ZMod q) (s : (ZMod ell)ˣ),
  e (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q s) = 1 →
    ∀ (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ) (b : ZMod q),
      (∀ (g : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
          (Catalan.A3.unitResidueCoordinates p q ell red e) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q u) g =

                      (Catalan.UnitModule.integralUnitPow p (Catalan.A3.F p) u Theta)) =
                  s ^ Theta.coeff g * w ^ q

@Catalan.Thaine.epsilon_inverse_root : ∀ {K : Type u_1} [inst : Field K] (z w : K),
  z ≠ 0 →
    w ≠ 0 →
      ∀ (a : ℕ) (m : ℤ),
        z ^ (2 * m + ↑a - 1) = 1 → z⁻¹ ^ m * (z⁻¹ ^ a - w) / (z⁻¹ - w) = z ^ m * (z ^ a - w⁻¹) / (z - w⁻¹)
'Catalan.Thaine.epsilon_inverse_root' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@Catalan.Thaine.epsilon_pair_inverse_root : ∀ {K : Type u_1} [inst : Field K] (z w : K),
  z ≠ 0 →
    w ≠ 0 →
      ∀ (a : ℕ) (m : ℤ),
        z ^ (2 * m + ↑a - 1) = 1 →
          z⁻¹ ^ m * (z⁻¹ ^ a - w) / (z⁻¹ - w) * (z⁻¹ ^ m * (z⁻¹ ^ a - w⁻¹) / (z⁻¹ - w⁻¹)) =
            z ^ m * (z ^ a - w) / (z - w) * (z ^ m * (z ^ a - w⁻¹) / (z - w⁻¹))
'Catalan.Thaine.epsilon_pair_inverse_root' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
Catalan.Thaine.epsilon_norm_factor_eq_one : ∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K] (p ell : ℕ) [inst_1 : Fact (Nat.Prime p)]
  [Fact (Nat.Prime ell)] (z : K),
  IsPrimitiveRoot z p →
    ∀ (a : (ZMod p)ˣ) (m : ℤ),
      z ^ (2 * m + ↑(↑a).val - 1) = 1 →
        ell ≡ 1 [MOD p] ∨ ell + 1 ≡ 0 [MOD p] →
          (z ^ m) ^ (ell - 1) * Polynomial.eval (z ^ (↑a).val) (Polynomial.cyclotomic ell K) /
              Polynomial.eval z (Polynomial.cyclotomic ell K) =
            1
'Catalan.Thaine.epsilon_norm_factor_eq_one' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

      ell ≡ 1 [MOD p] ∨ ell + 1 ≡ 0 [MOD p] →

          ∀ (s : (ZMod ell)ˣ),
            (∀ (x : (ZMod ell)ˣ), x ∈ Subgroup.zpowers s) →

                        (Catalan.A3.integralAut tau) alpha = ↑eta * alpha ∧
                          (Algebra.norm (Catalan.A3.F p)) ↑↑eta = 1 ∧

  [NumberField F] [inst_2 : Field L] [NumberField L] [inst_4 : Algebra F L] [IsGalois F L]

Catalan.Thaine.residue_coboundary_of_uniformizer_decomposition : ∀ (R : Type u_1) (K : Type u_2) (k : Type u_3)
  [inst : CommRing R] [IsDomain R] [inst_2 : Field K] [inst_3 : Field k] [inst_4 : Algebra R K] [IsFractionRing R K]
  (tau : R ≃+* R) (sigma : K ≃+* K),
  (∀ (x : R), sigma ((algebraMap R K) x) = (algebraMap R K) (tau x)) →
    ∀ (red : R →+* k),
      (∀ (x : R), red (tau x) = red x) →
        ∀ (pi : R),
          pi ≠ 0 →
            ∀ (t u eta : Rˣ),
              tau pi = ↑t * pi →
                ∀ (alpha : K) (n : ℤ),
                  alpha = (algebraMap R K) ↑u * (algebraMap R K) pi ^ n →
                    sigma alpha = (algebraMap R K) ↑eta * alpha → red ↑eta = red ↑t ^ n

Catalan.Thaine.exists_residue_extension_of_inertia_one : ∀ (F : Type u_1) (L : Type u_2) (k : Type u_3) [inst : Field F]
  [NumberField F] [inst_2 : Field L] [NumberField L] [inst_4 : Field k] [inst_5 : Algebra F L]
  (v : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers F))
  (P : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) [P.asIdeal.LiesOver v.asIdeal],

                ∀ (x : NumberField.RingOfIntegers F),
                  redL ((algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers F) (NumberField.RingOfIntegers L)) x) = red x

      (P : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p ell)))
      [P.asIdeal.LiesOver v.asIdeal],
      ↑ell ∈ v.asIdeal →
        ∀ (red : NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p) →+* ZMod ell),
          Function.Surjective ⇑red →

              ∃ redB,
                Function.Surjective ⇑redB ∧

                        ∀ (s : (ZMod ell)ˣ), redB (Catalan.Thaine.auxiliaryUniformizerRatio p ell s) = ↑s

  [IsDedekindDomain R] [inst_2 : Field k] (P : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum R) (tau : R ≃+* R),
  (∀ (x : R), tau x ∈ P.asIdeal ↔ x ∈ P.asIdeal) →
    ∀ (red : R →+* k),

  [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    p ≠ ell →
      ell ≡ 1 [MOD p] ∨ ell + 1 ≡ 0 [MOD p] →

          ∀ (s : (ZMod ell)ˣ),

                  (∀ (sigma : Gal(↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p ell)/Catalan.A3.F p)),
                      Ideal.map (Catalan.A3.integralAut sigma).toRingHom (Ideal.span {alpha}) = Ideal.span {alpha}) ∧

Catalan.Thaine.exists_circular_class_relation : ∀ (p q ell : ℕ) [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime ell)],
  p ≠ 2 →
    p ≠ ell →
      q ∣ ell - 1 →
        ell ≡ 1 [MOD p] ∨ ell + 1 ≡ 0 [MOD p] →
          ∀ d ∈ Catalan.Thaine.realCircularUnits p,
            ∀ (s : (ZMod ell)ˣ),
              (∀ (x : (ZMod ell)ˣ), x ∈ Subgroup.zpowers s) →
                ∃ m,
                  Function.HasFiniteSupport m ∧
                    (∀ (v : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))),
                        ↑ell ∉ v.asIdeal → m v = 0) ∧
                      ∑ᶠ (v : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))),
                            m v •
                              Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q
                                ((ClassGroup.mk (Catalan.A3.F p)) (FractionalIdealGroup.prime v)) =
                          0 ∧
                        ∀ (v : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p)))
                          (P :
                            IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (NumberField.RingOfIntegers ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p ell))),
                          P.asIdeal.LiesOver v.asIdeal →
                            ↑ell ∈ v.asIdeal →
                              ∀ (red : NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p) →+* ZMod ell),
                                Function.Surjective ⇑red → RingHom.ker red = v.asIdeal → red ↑(d ^ 2) = ↑s ^ m v

  (red : NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p) →+* ZMod ell)
  (e : Catalan.UnitQuotient.PowerQuotient (ZMod ell)ˣ q ≃ₗ[ZMod q] ZMod q)
  (u : (NumberField.RingOfIntegers (Catalan.A3.F p))ˣ) (b : ZMod q),
  (∀ (g : Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
      (Catalan.A3.unitResidueCoordinates p q ell red e) (Catalan.UnitQuotient.powerClass q u) g =
        (MonoidAlgebra.coeff 1) g - b) →
    ∀ (Theta : Catalan.R p (Catalan.A3.F p)),
      Catalan.Thaine.groupAlgebraOfFunction (ZMod q) (Catalan.G p (Catalan.A3.F p))

  7 ≤ p →
    q ≠ 2 →
      ¬q ∣ (p - 1) / 2 →

                      Catalan.UnitModule.topUnitAnn p (↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p p)) q →
                    ↑q ∣ Catalan.weight p (↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p p)) Theta →
                      ∃ c ∈ Catalan.Circular.circularUnits p ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p p),
                        ∃ b,
                          Catalan.upow p (↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p p)) (Catalan.xmζ p (↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p p)) x ⋯)
                              ((1 + MonoidAlgebra.single (Catalan.ι p ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p p)) 1) * Theta) =
                            (Units.map
                                  ↑(algebraMap (NumberField.RingOfIntegers ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p p))
                                      ↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p p)))
                                c *
                              b ^ q
'Catalan.Thaine.literal_xm_zeta_plus_circular_power' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

    q ≠ 2 →
      ¬q ∣ (p - 1) / 2 →

                        Catalan.UnitModule.topUnitAnn p (↥(Catalan.A3.Bsub p p)) q →

                            b ^ q

Catalan.Thaine.pure_power_of_primary_powers : ∀ (p q : ℕ) [inst : Fact (Nat.Prime p)] [inst_1 : Fact (Nat.Prime q)]

  ¬q ∣ Nat.card (Catalan.G p K) →
    ∀ (a : Kˣ) (I : Ideal (MonoidAlgebra (ZMod q) (Catalan.G p K))),

          (Catalan.Runge.reduceFull p K q) Psi ∈ I →

          (Catalan.Runge.reduceFull p K q) Theta ∈ I * Catalan.UnitModule.bottomUnitAnn p K q ⋯ →

Catalan.Thaine.primary_of_localized_unit_power : ∀ (A : Type u_1) (D : Type u_2) (K : Type u_3) [inst : CommRing A]
  [inst_1 : CommRing D] [IsDomain D] [IsIntegrallyClosed D] [inst_4 : Field K] [inst_5 : Algebra A D]
  [inst_6 : Algebra D K] [inst_7 : Algebra A K] [IsScalarTower A D K] [IsFractionRing D K] (q : ℕ),
  0 < q →
    ∀ (red : D →+* Catalan.UnitQuotient.ModSquare A q),
      (∀ (a : A), red ((algebraMap A D) a) = (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span {↑q ^ 2})) a) →
        ∀ (v : Dˣ) (c : Aˣ) (b : Kˣ),
          (Units.map ↑(algebraMap D K)) v = (Units.map ↑(algebraMap A K)) c * b ^ q →
            (Units.map ↑red) v = 1 → c ∈ Catalan.UnitQuotient.primaryUnits A q
